1b1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya many+dzu shrI ba ri prats+tshi na ma ma hA yA na sU tra/bod skad du 
1b2(mdo sna tshogs): 'phags pa 'jam dpal gyis dris pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo//sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems 
1b3(mdo sna tshogs): dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das 
1b4(mdo sna tshogs): mnyan du yod pa'i kun nas mdzes pa'i khang pa na/dge slong brgya phrag phyed dang bcu gsum gyi dge slong gi 
1b5(mdo sna tshogs): dge 'dun chen po dang /dpag tu med pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dang /'dul ba brgya 
1b6(mdo sna tshogs): stong mang po dang /spyan ras gzigs dbang po la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' chen po 
1b7(mdo sna tshogs): rnams dang /thabs gcig tu bzhugs te/bcom ldan 'das rin po che'i seng ge'i khri la sku mi g.yo bar 
1b8(mdo sna tshogs): bzhugs pa dang /de nas byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po 'jam dpal sangs rgyas kyi 
2a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//mthus stan las langs te/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu byas nas pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar nas bcom ldan 'das 
2a2(mdo sna tshogs): la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das bsod nams chen po gang gis 'dul ba bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du ma'i bsam pa yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ba de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos kyi drung du 'gyur ba'i 
2a3(mdo sna tshogs): bsod nams chen po de ji snyed tshad ji tsam lags/de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'jam dpal la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//'jam dpal 
2a4(mdo sna tshogs): bsod nams chen po gang gi 'dul ba bye ba khrag khrigs brgya stong gi bsam pa yongs su rdzogs bar byed pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i chosakyi drung du 'gyur ba'i bsod nams chen po de ni shes rab las byung ba/
2a5(mdo sna tshogs): snying rje chen pos yongs su bsgos pa yin te bsam gyis mi khyab po//'jam dpal 'dzam bu'i gling gi sems can ji snyed pa de dag thams cad dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam dang ldan par gyur la/gang sems 
2a6(mdo sna tshogs): can de dag thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi phung por gyur pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po de brgyar bsgyur bas gling bzhi la dbang po rin po che bdun dang ldan pa'i 'khor los bsgyur ba'i rgyal por 'gyur te/de'i rin 
2a7(mdo sna tshogs): po che bdun 'di lta ste/'khor lo rin po che dang /glang po rin po che dang /rta rin po che dang /nor bu rin po che dang /bud med rin po che dang /khyim bdag rin po che dang /blon po rin po che dang 'di bdun 'byung ngo /
2a8(mdo sna tshogs): de la bu stong po pa/rtul phod ba/yan lag mchog gi gzugs dang ldan pa/pha rol gyi sde rab tu 'joms pa rnams yod de/'jam dpal de ltar 'khor los bsgyur ba'i rgyalapo de rdzu 'phrul che zhing 
2b1(mdo sna tshogs): mthu che ba yin no//'jam dpal gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi sems can ji snyed pa de dag thams cad 'khor los bsgyur ba'i bsod nams kyi phung po dang ldan par 'gyur la/gang sems can de dag thams 
2b2(mdo sna tshogs): cad kyi bsod nams kyi phung por gyur pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po de brgyar bsgyur bas brgya byin lha'i dbang por 'gyur te/de ltar lha'i dbang po ni rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che'o//'jam dpal gling bzhipa'i 'jig rten gyi khams
2b3(mdo sna tshogs): kyi sems can ji snyed pa dedag thams cad brgya byin gyis bsod nams kyi phung po dag ldan par 'gyur la/gang soms can de dag thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi phungapor gyur pa'i bsod namasakyi phung po de brgya stong du 
2b4(mdo sna tshogs): bsgyur bas gang 'dod pa'i khams su bstan pas go bar byed pa 'dod pa'i khams kyi dbang po bdud du 'gyur te/de ltar 'dod pa'i khams kyi dbangapo bdud ni rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthuche'o//'jam dpal gling pabazhi pa'i 
2b5(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten gyi khams na sems can ji snyed pa de dag thams cad bdud kyi bsod nams kyi phungapa dang ldan par gyur la/gang sems can de dag thams can kyi bsod nams kyi phung por gyur pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po
2b6(mdo sna tshogs): de brgya stong du bsgyur pas gang 'jig rten gyi khams stong byams pas khyab pa stong gi bdag po tshangs par 'gyur ro//'jam dpal stong gi 'jig rten gyi khams na sem can ji snyed pa de dag thabs cad stong gi bdag 
2b7(mdo sna tshogs): po tshangs pa'i bsod nams dang ldan par gyur la//gang sems can de dag thabs cad kyi bsod nams su gyur pa'i bsod nams kyis phung po de brgya stong du bsgyur bas gang stong gnyis kyi 'jig rten gyi khams byams 
2b8(mdo sna tshogs): pas khyab pa stong gnyis kyi bdag po tshangs par 'gyur//'jam dpal stong gnyis kyi 'jig rtenagyi khams na sems can ji snyad pa de dag thams cad stong gnyis kyi bdag po tshongs pa'i bsod nams kyi phungapo dang
3a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//ldan par gyur la/gang sems can de dag thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi phung por gyur pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po de brgya stong du bsgyur pas/gang stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 
3a2(mdo sna tshogs): byams pas khyab pa tshangs pa stong gsum gyi stong chen pa'i 'jig rten gyi bdag po dbang phyug chen po'i mchog tu 'gyur ro//'jam dpal tshangs pa dbang phyug chen po mchog la bltos/nam bskal pa 'jig pa na bskal 
3a3(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i chu 'byung ba de'i tshe/phyis par gyi bskal pa gcig tu char 'bab cing chu'i thigs pa 'byung ba des stong gsum gyi stong chen pe'i 'jig rten gyi khams chus gang bar byed de/tshangs pa'i 'jig rten gyi bar du chus gang bar 
3a4(mdo sna tshogs): 'gyur ba'i chu'i thigs pa de dag thams cad kyang tshangs pa dbang phyug chenapo'i mchog des shes so//de ltar ye shes chen po dang ldan zhing rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba yin te/dge ba'i rtsa ba chu dus ni dbang phyug chen por 
3a5(mdo sna tshogs): mi 'gyur ro//'jam dpalastong gsum gyi stong chen po 'i 'jig rten gyi khams na sems can ji snyed pa de dag thams cad stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i bdag po tshangs pa chen po'i bsod nams kyi phung po dang ldan 
3a6(mdo sna tshogs): par gyur la/gang sems can de dag thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi phung por gyur pa'i bsod nams kyi phung bo de bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du mar bsgyur pas mthu chen po thob pa'i rang sangs rgyas su 'gyur ro//
3a7(mdo sna tshogs): 'jam dpal stong gsum gyi stong chen pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams lta re shig zhog ste/'jam dpal sangs rgyas kyi yul gyi mtha' phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams nas sems can ji snyed pa de de dag thams cad rang 
3a8(mdo sna tshogs): sangs rgyas mthu chen po thob pa'i bsod nams dang ldan par gyur la/gang sems cad de dag thams cad kyi bso nams kyi phung por gyur ba'i bsod nams kyi phung po'i bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong /du mar bsgyur bas 
3b1(mdo sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpa' srid pa'i tha mar gyur pa gcig tu 'gyur ro//'jam dpal nam mkha'i khams kyi mtha' phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams na sems can sgo nga las skyes pa dang /mngal nas skyes pa dang /
3b2(mdo sna tshogs): drod gsher las skyes pa dang /rdzus te skyes pa dang /gzugs yod pa dang /gzugs med pa dang /'du shes yod pa dang /'du shes med pa dang /'du she med 'du shes med min gyi sems can ji snyed pa de dag 
3b3(mdo sna tshogs): thams cad byang chub sems dpa' srid ba'i tha mar gyur pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po dang ldan par gyur la/gang sems can de dag thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi phung por gyur pa'i bsod nams gyi phung po de bye ba khrag 
3b4(mdo sna tshogs): khrig brgya stong du mar bsgyur bas de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku la spu'i khung bu gcig tu 'gyur ro//de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa'i skul spu'i khung bu brgya stong phrag dgu bcu rtsa dgu re re nas 'grub par 'gyur te/
3b5(mdo sna tshogs): 'jam dpal gang spu'i khung bu thams cad du gtogs pa'i bsod nams kyi phung bor gyur pa'i bsod nams 'gyur ro//de bzhin du/de bzhin du de bzhin gsheg pa'i sku la dpe byad bzang po gcig 
3b6(mdo sna tshogs): tu 'gyur ro//de bzhin de de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i dpe byad bzang pos brgyad cu re re nas 'grub par gyur te/'jam dpal dpe byad bzang por brgyad cur gtogs pa'i gang bsod nams kyi phung po ji snyed pa'i 
3b7(mdo sna tshogs): bsod nams kyi phung po de bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du mar bsgyur/bas de bzhin gshegs pa'i phyag gam zhabs gcig ri mo'i rjes kyi mtshan du 'gyur ro//de ltar ri mo'i rjes kyi mtshan brgyad cu la 'di lta ste/
3b8(mdo sna tshogs): gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan dang /dpal be'u dang /phreng ba dang /kyo ba thad dang /cod pan dang /dbyig to dang /bum pa dang /glang po dang /rta dang /stag dang /chu srin dang /nya dang /ru spal dang /rma bya dang /ka la bing ka dang /
4a1(mdo sna tshogs): shang shang te'u dang /tsa sha dang /dur ba dang /ne tso dang /dad pa dang /thi ba dang /nas dang /sman chen po dang /'o dam dang /ba men dang /klu dang /ra dang /khyu mchog dang /ri dang /bil ba dang /shing dang ri dgas nag 
4a2(mdo sna tshogs): po dang /nor bu rin po che dang /ral gri mchog dang /rdo rje dang /gzhu dang /mda' dang /shag ti dang /mdung rtse gsum pa dang /gshol dang /gtun dang /sta re dang /zhags pa dang /gru dang /mu tig gi rgyan dang /sprin dang /
4a3(mdo sna tshogs): tshangs pa dang /dbang po dang /'khor ba srung po dang /chu'i lha dang /'phags skyes po dang /mig mi bzang dang /nor sbyin dang /drang srong chen po dang /dpal dang /nyi ma dang /zla ba dang /me dang /rlung dang /pad mo dang /g.yung drung 
4a4(mdo sna tshogs): dang /gru gsum dang /dge ba'i bstan dang /me long dang /rnga yab dang /rtsa dur ba dang /pu ro da sha dang /khye'u dang /bu mo dang /rnga dang /dung dang /smrig dang /ga dang /gdu bu dang /dpung rgyan dang /rna cha dang /sor gdub dang /rna cha phyang phul 
4a5(mdo sna tshogs): can dang /me tog bzang po dang /dpag bsam gyi shing dang /'khor lo dang /dpung gi seng ge dang /'di rnams ni ri mo'i rjes kyi mtshan brgyad cu yin te/'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i phyag dang zhabs kyi mthil la byung ngo //
4a6(mdo sna tshogs): 'jam dpal gang mtshan brgyad cu'i ri mor gtogs pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po de bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du mar bsgyur bas de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku la skyes bu chen po'i mtshan 
4a7(mdo sna tshogs): gcig tu 'gyur te/de bzhin du skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis ra rer 'gyur ro//'di lta ste/dbu'i gtsug tor dang /dbu'i mthon mthing la g.yas phyogs su 'khyil pa dang /dpral ba mnyam pa dang /kha dog 
4a8(mdo sna tshogs): gis brgyan pa dang /smin mtshams kyi mdzod spu dang /spyan mthon mthing la ba'i rdzi ma 'dra ba dang /so bzhi bcu thags bzang ba dang /mche ba dkar ba dang /'gram pa seng ge'i 'dra ba dang /lce che zhing srab pa dang /ro stong 
4b1(mdo sna tshogs): seng ge 'dra ba/shing n+ya gro d+ha ltar chu zheng gab pa dang /spu khung re re nas skyes pa dang /pho mtshan mi snang bar nub pa dang /brla gang zhing zlum pa dang /byin pa ri dgas kyi rgyal po e ne ya 'dra ba dang /zhabs kyi rting pa che ba dang /
4b2(mdo sna tshogs): zhabs dang phyag gi mthil 'jam zhing mnyen pa dang /zhabs dang phyag gi sor mo'i bar dra par 'brel ba dang /zhabs dang phyag go sor mo ring ba dang /zhabs kyi steng mtho ba dang /ro bro ba'i mchog dang /dpung mgo bzlum pa 
4b3(mdo sna tshogs): dang /bdun mtho ba dang /lpags pa srab la gser gyi kha dog 'dra ba dang /ma btud pa phyag pus mo'i lha da la reg pa dang /zhabs rab tu gnas pa dang /zhabs dang phyug gi mthil na 'khor lo'i mtshan yod pa dang /
4b4(mdo sna tshogs): tshangs pa'i dbyangs dang /'di dag ni skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis yin te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i skul 'byung ngo //'jam dpal skyes bu chen po'i mtshan su bcu rtsa gnyis su gtogs pa'i bsod 
4b5(mdo sna tshogs): nams kyi phung por gang gyur pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po de grangs med par bsgyur ba dang /bsam gyis mi khyab par bsgyur ba dang /dpag tu med par bsgyur ba dang brjod du med par bsgyur bas de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
4b6(mdo sna tshogs): chos kyi drung du 'gyur ro//chos kyi dung des 'dul ba'i dbang du de bzhin gshegs pas 'jig rten gyi khams tshad med grangs med par dbyangs kyi rig par byed do//dbyangs kyis ji lta ba de bzhin du 'od dang skus kyang 
4b7(mdo sna tshogs): de bzhin no//'jam dpal de ltar shes rab chen po las byung ba snying rje chen pos yongs su bsgos pa/thabs mkhas pa dang smon lam gyis bskyed pa/tshul khrims yongs su dag pa bsgoms pa'i khyad par 
4b8(mdo sna tshogs): las yang dag par skyes pa/bsod nams chen po 'di dag ni nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis bsam gyis mi khyab bo//'jam dpal rgyu gnyis kyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzugs kyi sku khyad 
5a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//par du 'phags pa yin no//rgyu gnyis gang zhe na/smon lam gyi dbang dang /'dul ba'i sems can rnams kyi dge ba yongs su smin pa'i dbang ste/'jam dpal rgyu de gnyis kyis na de bzhin 
5a2(mdo sna tshogs): gshegs pa'i gzugs kyi sku khyad par du 'phags pa yin no////'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzugs kyi sku khyed par du 'phags pa ji lta ba de bzhin du chos bstan pa yang khyad par du 'phags pa yi na/'jam 
5a3(mdo sna tshogs): dpal de bzhin gshegs pa chos bstan pa ji lta ba de bzhin du'od kyang khyad par du 'phags pa min no//'jam dpal ji ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od khyad par du 'phags pa de bzhin du spyod pa yang khyad 
5a4(mdo sna tshogs): par du 'phags pa yin no/ /'jam dpal de bzhin gshes pa ni skus 'jig rten thams cad du khyab pa yin te/'jam dpal kha dog dang /dbyibs dang /spyod pa'i bye brag gang dang gang gis sems can rnams 
5a5(mdo sna tshogs): 'dul bar 'gyur ba'i kha dog dang /dbyibs dag spyod pa'i bye brag de dang de lta bur de bzhin gshegs pa sems can rnams kyis mthong ngo //'jam dpal mtshan gyi bye brag gang dang gang gi sems can rnams 
5a6(mdo sna tshogs): 'dul par 'gyur ba'i mtshan gyi bye brag de dang de lta bu de bzhin gshegs pa semasacan rnams kyis mthong ngo//'jam dpal chos bstan pa gang dang gang gis semasacan rnams yongs su smin par 'gyur ba'i 
5a7(mdo sna tshogs): chos bstan pa de dang de lta bur de bzhig gshegs pa chos bstan par sems can rnams kyis shes so//'jam dpal spyod pa gang gis sems can rnam de bzhin gshegs pa'i bstan pa la 'jug pa dang 'dul bar 
5a8(mdo sna tshogs): 'gyur ba'i spyod pa de dang de la de bzhin gshegs pa gnas par sems can gyis mthong ngo //'jam dpa' de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'jig rten du 
5b1(mdo sna tshogs): 'byung bar 'gyur ba yang skye bo mang po la phan pa dang /skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /'jig rten la snying brtse ba dang /lha dang mi rnams dang /skye bo phal po che'i don dang /phan pa dang bde ba'i phyir skye'o//de nas byang chub sems 
5b2(mdo sna tshogs): dpa' 'jam dpal stan las langs te/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das bdag gi ston pa mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa dang 
5b3(mdo sna tshogs): gang zag zla med pa/khams gsum na mgon po'i mchog sems can thams cad la phan par mdzad pa/'jig rten gyi chos kyis mi bskyod pa/nam mkha' ltar mi gos pa/bsam gyis mi khyab pa/bsam par 'os 
5b4(mdo sna tshogs): pa/blta 'dod pa/blta na sdug pa 'di 'dra ba ni bdag gis rnyed pa rab tu rnyed do//bde bar gshegs pa rnyed pa rab tu rnyed do //bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal nas/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa 
5b5(mdo sna tshogs): dga'o//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag dang /dge slong de dag bcom ldan 'das kyis bshad pa la mngon par bstod do//'phags pa 'jam dpal gyis dris pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i

5b7(mdo sna tshogs): chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//de nas dmigs pa'i lta ba can rnam par rtog pa dang bcas shing rnam par dpyod pa dang bcas pa'i mu stegs pa de dag rnams theg pa chen po'i nang du song ste/
5b8(mdo sna tshogs): zhes dang bcas shing thal mo sbyar nas bdag med pa'i dri ba dag yongs su dris pa/rigs kyi bu thams cad mkhyen pas ni lus la bdag med do zhes gsungs la/gal te lus la yang dag par bdag med na ji ltar na

6b1(mdo sna tshogs): brjod par mi bya'o//mu stegs pa rnams kyis smras pa/'o na 'dir gang zhig dmigs par 'gyur/theg pa chen po pa rnams kyis smras pa/dmigs par gyur pa ni ci yang med de//mu stegs pa rnams kyi smras pa/ci na ma 
6b2(mdo sna tshogs): mkha' ltar stong pa nyid yin nam/theg pa chen po pa rnamasakyis smras pa/grogs po dag de de bzhin te/nam mkha' ltar stong pa nyid yin//mu stegs pa rnams kyisasmras pa/gal te de ltar yin na rtse ba dang /rgod 
6b3(mdo sna tshogs): pa dang /du ba dang /rol pa dang /khro ba dang /nga rgyal dang phrag dog dang /phra ma la sogs pa ji ltar blta bar bgyi/theg pa chen po pa rnam kyis smras pa/sgyu ma dang /rmi lam dang /mig 'phrul dang 'dra ba yin no//mu stegs 
6b4(mdo sna tshogs): pa rnams kyis smras pa/sgyu ma ni ji lta bu lags/rmi lam dang /mig 'khrul ni ji lta bu lags/theg pa chen po pa rnamasagyis smras pa/sgyu min mtshon pa tsam mo//rmi lam ni so sor snang ba tsam ste/gzung bar bya ba 
6b5(mdo sna tshogs): ma yin zhing rang bzhin gyis stong pa/yod pa ma yin pa'i ngo bo nyid do//mig 'phrul ni bcos ma tsam du dgos pa'o//grogs po dag de de bzhinate/dngos po ji snyed pa thams cad ni sgyu ma dang /rmi lam dang/mig 
6b6(mdo sna tshogs): 'phrul dang 'dra bar shes par bya'o//gzhan yang kun rdzob dang /don dam pa dag bstan par bya ste/de la kun rdzob ces bya ba ni 'di ni bdag go/'di ni gzhan no zhes bya ba dang /srog dang /skyes bu dang /gang zag dang /
6b7(mdo sna tshogs): byed pa po dang /tshor ba po dang /nor dang /bu dang /chung ma dang /mdza' bshes dang /nye du la sogs par rtog pa de ni kun rdzob ces bya'o//gang la bdag med pa dang /gzhan med pa dang /srog med pa dang /skyes bu med pa dang /
6b8(mdo sna tshogs): gang zag med pa dang /byed pa po med pa dang /tshor ba po med pa dang /nor med pa dang /bu med pa dang /chung ma med pa dang /mdza' bshes med pa dang /nye du la sogs med pa de ni don dam pa zhes bya'o//gang la rang bzhin 
7a1(mdo sna tshogs): gyis dngos po thams cad du yongs su brtags shing dge ba dang /mi dge ba'i 'bras bu dang /skye ba dang /'gag pa ni kun rdzob bo//dge ba dang /mi dge ba'i'abras bu med pa dang /skye ba med pa dang /'gag pa 
7a2(mdo sna tshogs): med pa de bzhin nyid kyi ngo bo nyid de la ni kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /rnam par byang ba med de/de ni dbu ma'i chos rnams kyi sgrub pa lhur len pa'o//de la 'di skad ces bya ste/kun rdzob dang ni don dam pa ste/dbye ba gnyis su yang 
7a3(mdo sna tshogs): dag bshad//kun rdzob 'jig rten pa'i chos//don dam 'jig rten 'das pa'o//kun rdzob chos la zhugs pa yi//sems can nyon mongs dbang du 'gro//don dam yongs su ma shes pas//'khor bar yun ring 'khor bar 
7a4(mdo sna tshogs): 'gyur//kun rdzob 'jig rten chos rnams la//mi mkhas pa dag rtog par byed//kun du rtog pa de yis ni//sdug bsngal nyams su myong bar 'gyur//ji ltar so so'i skye bo yi//byis pas grol lam mi shes pas//mi zad 
7a5(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i sdug bsngal dag/mang po rjes su myong bar 'gyur/gang du srid pa 'gag 'gyur ba'i//don dam dag ni mi shes pas//skye ba dang ni 'gag 'gyur zhing //skye med 'gro dang 'ong bar 'gyur//'jig rten chos la gnas pa 
7a6(mdo sna tshogs): yi//blun po 'khor lo bzhin du 'khor//sdug bsngal gnas can 'khor ba 'dir//yang dang yang du 'khor bar 'gyur//ji ltar nyi ma zla ba dag/slar yang 'od zhing 'gro bar byed//de bzhin srid par 'pho ba na//slar yang 
7a7(mdo sna tshogs): 'od zhing 'gro bar 'gyur//'khor ba thams cad mi rtag cing //mi brtan skad gcig 'jig pa nyid//de bas don dam shes pa yis//kun rdzob bden pa'i gnas spong ngo //mtho ris gnas nas lha rnams dang //dri za lha mi 
7a8(mdo sna tshogs): rnams kyang ni//kun la' pho ba yod gyur pa//kun kyang kun rdzob 'bras bu yin//grub dang rigs 'dzin gnod sbyin dang //dri za dang ni lto 'phye rnams//slar yang dmyal bar 'gro 'gyur ba//kun kyang kun rdzob 'bral bu 
7b1(mdo sna tshogs): yin//gang zhig brtson drag lha rnams dang //yon tan 'byung gnas la gnas gang //mtho ris las 'pho ltung gyur pa//thams cad kun rdzob 'bras bu yin//brgya byin 'khor los bsgyur nyid de//gang gis dam 
7b2(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i gnas thob nas//slar yang byol song skye gnas 'jug/thams cad kun rdzob 'bral bu yin//de bas mtho ris lha rnams kyi//bden pa bzang po kun spong la//byang chub sems ni 'od gsal ba//rnal 'byor 
7b3(mdo sna tshogs): pas ni rtag tu bsgom//dngos po med cing dmigs su med//thams cad stong pa gnas med pa//spros pa rnams las yang dag 'das//byang chub sems dpa'i mtshan nyid yin//sra ba ma yin 'jam pa min//
7b4(mdo sna tshogs): dro ba ma yin grang ba min//de ni reg min gzung bya min//byang chub sems kyi mtshan nyid yin//ring po ma yin thung pa min//zlum po ma yin gru gsum min//phra ba ma yin sbom pa 'ang min//byang chub sems kyis mtshan 
7b5(mdo sna tshogs): nyid yin//sgom pa rnams las rnam 'das shing //mu stegs rnams kyi spyod yul min//shes rab pha rol phyin sbyor ba//byang chub sems kyi mtshams nyid yin//dpe med bsgom du med pa dang //mthong min gnas kyi 
7b6(mdo sna tshogs): mchog gyur la//rang bzhin gyis ni rnam dag pa//byang chub sems kyi mtshang nyid yin//thams cad lbu ba lta bu ste//chu bur lta bur snying po med//rtag pa ma yin bdag med pa//sgyu ma mig sgyu dag dang mtshungs//gong bu 
7b7(mdo sna tshogs): bzhin du bsdus gyur pa//spros pa rnams kyis yongs su gang //'dod chags zhe sdang la sogs ltar//de ni sgyu mtshungs 'ba' zhig yin//ji ltar nang na thig le ni//skad cig de la mi mthong ste//shes rab pha rol phyin
7b8(mdo sna tshogs):  mthong na//de bzhin blo ni 'du ma byas//rtag tu rgod cing rtse ba dang //smra zhing gru dang rol mo dang //gzhi la sogs pa'i bden pa rnams//de dag thams cad rmi lam mtshungs//lus can kun gyis 'du byas pa//'di kun rmi lam 
8a1(mdo sna tshogs): dang mtshungs la//rmi lam sems kyi kun rtog ste//sems kyang nam mkha' lta bu yin//gang zhig tshul 'di rtag sgom pa//shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i tshul//gang zhig 'di ni rtag sgom pa//dngos po kun 
8a2(mdo sna tshogs): las rnam grol nas//go 'phang mchog ni thob par 'gyur//bla na med pa'i byang chub gang //sangs rgyas kun gyis rnam bsgoms pa//bsgoms dang bcas pas 'dzin na ni//theg chen 'bras bu 'thob par 'gyur//'phags pa 
8a3(mdo sna tshogs): bdag med pa dris pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo rdzogs s+ho////rgya gar gyi mkhan po ka ma la gub ta dang /zhu chen gyi lo ts+tsha ba dge slong rin chen bzang pos bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab bo////
8a4(mdo sna tshogs): 'phags pa 'jig rten 'dzin gyis yongs su dris pa zhes bya ba'i mdo//bam po dang po//sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 
8a5(mdo sna tshogs): 'das rgyal po'i khab bya ka lan dka'i gnas 'od ma'i tshal na/dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs so//de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das 'khor mang po brgya stong snyed kyis yongs su bskor ba la chos ston to//
8a6(mdo sna tshogs): 'khor de'i nang na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'jig rten 'dzin ces bya ba'ang 'dus par gyur te/byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po rnams tshad med pa'i yon tan gyis yongs su brgyan pas 
8a7(mdo sna tshogs): sems bskyed pa'i phyir/chos thams cad kyi nges pa'i don mthar phyin pa thams cad shes par bya ba'i phyir tshad med pa'i smon pas tshad med pa'i rgyan yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ba shin tu shes par bya ba'i phyir/tshad 
8a8(mdo sna tshogs): med pa'i chos rnams kyi nges pa'i mtshan nyid rtogs shing khong du chud par bya ba'i phyir/smon lam tshad med pas lhag pa'i bsam pa shin tu rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir/yongs su rdzogs par shes pa'i phyir/sbyin pa'i 
8b1(mdo sna tshogs): rgyan dang /nges pa'i rnam par dag pa'i phyir/tshul khrims kyi brgyan dang /bzod pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir/mnyen cing nges pa'i sems dang /rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir/brtson 'grus dang /yongs su 
8b2(mdo sna tshogs): dag pa shes par bya ba'i phyir/bsam gtan dang shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa khong du chud cing rtogs par bya ba dang /de dag la sogs pa yon tan tshad med pa'i phyir 'dug pa las langs nas/bla gos phyogs gcig tu 
8b3(mdo sna tshogs): byas te/bcom ldan 'das gang na ba de logs su thal mo sbyar nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das bdag skye bo mang po la phan pa dang /bde ba'i slad du/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
8b4(mdo sna tshogs): chen po rnams sangs rgyas kyi gdung mi gcod par 'gyur ba dang /spyod lam kun du tshul khrims la ma chags shing /tshul khrims rnam par dag pa dang ldan pa dang /chos chen po la nan tan byed cing 'phags pa'i chos 
8b5(mdo sna tshogs): kyi spyod yul tshad med pa 'dzin pa la shin du mkhas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi slad du bdag zhu ba zhu'o//bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po dag chos rnams kyi yang 
8b6(mdo sna tshogs): dag pa'i mtshan nyid la shin du mkhas pa lags/ji ltar na chos rnams kyi mtshan nyid shin du rnam par 'byed pa la mkhas pa lags/ji ltar ni dran pa'i mthu dang ldan pa lags/ji ltar na cho sa thams cad kyi rnam grangs 
8b7(mdo sna tshogs): rnam par 'byed pa la mkhas/ji ltar na lus 'di bor nas nam bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi bar du rgyun mi 'chad pa'i dran pa dang ldan pa lags/de skad ces 
8b8(mdo sna tshogs): gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten 'dzin la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//legs so legs so//'jig rten 'dzin de ltar khyod kyis byang chub sems dpa'i sems dpa' chen po 
9a1(mdo sna tshogs): rnams kyi phyir/de bzhin gshegs pa la don de dag dris pa ni legs so//khyod skye bo mang po la phan pa dang bde ba'i phyir/'jig rten la snying brtse ba'i phyir/lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten la phan pa dang bde ba'i don 
9a2(mdo sna tshogs): kyi phyir/gzhan yang da ltar gyi dus dang /ma 'ongs pa'i dus kyi byang chub sems dpa' la sogs pa la 'od chen pos snang bar bya ba'i phyir dri ba dris pa'i bsod nams ni dpag tu med do//khyod kyis de bzhin gshegs pa la don
9a3(mdo sna tshogs): 'di dag dris pa ni nges par sems can thams cad kyi the tshom yongs su gcad pa'i phyir/sems can thams cad 'od kyis snang bar bya ba dang /bsrung bar bya ba'i phyir/sems can dag la phan pa'i don bstan pa'i phyir/skye bo dag 
9a4(mdo sna tshogs): gi yang za ba'i lam las bsgral bar bya ba'i phyir/skye bo mang po'i rten dang /gnas dang /mgon dang /skyabs bya ba'i phyir/ngan song gsum las gdon cing dbyung ba'i phyir/sems can dge bla na med pa'i lam la bzung ba'i 
9a5(mdo sna tshogs): phyir/skye bo mang po dag skye ba dang /rga ba dang /na ba dang /'chi ba dang /slos pa'i mya ngan dang /smre sngags 'don pa dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /'khrug pa las rnam par grol ba'i phyir/sems can la bla na med pa'i mya ngan las 'das 
9a6(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i bde ba sbyin pa'i phyir/phyi ma ma 'ongs pa'i dus na dam pa'i chos bsrung zhing bskyab pa'i phyir/phyi ma ma 'ongasapa 'jigs pa'i dus ngan pa'i sems can dag bsgral ba'i phyir/de bzhin gshegs pa la dri bar bsams pa 
9a7(mdo sna tshogs): legs so//de lta bas na 'jig rten 'dzin khyod legs par nyon la rab tu yid la zung shig dang /rnam par phye ste bshad par bya'o//bcom ldan 'das de ltar bgyi'o//zhes gsol nas byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten 
9a8(mdo sna tshogs): 'dzin bcom ldan 'das la phyir nyan to//bcom ldan 'das kyis de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//skyes bu dam pa de la 'di skad ces bya ste/phan pa'i don bzhi mthong ba'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams 
9b1(mdo sna tshogs): chos rnams kyi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid la brtson zhing nan tan byed pa dang /chos rnams kyi mtshan nyid rnam par 'byed pa la shin du mkhas par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/dran pa yongs su rdzogs pa thob par bya ba dang /dran pa rgyun 
9b2(mdo sna tshogs): mi 'chad pa thob par bya ba dang shin du byang ba'i shes rab kyis gong nas gong du 'phar ba dang /rtag tu dran pa'i sems dang ldan pa ste/'jig rten 'dzin 'di dag ni don rnam pa bzhi mthong ba'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
9b3(mdo sna tshogs): chen po rnams chos rnams kyi yang dag pa mtshan nyid la brtson zhing non tan byed pa dang /chosarnams kyi mtshan nyid rnam pa 'byed pa la shin du mkhasapa yin no//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang don bzhi mthong ba'i byang chub
9b4(mdo sna tshogs): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams chos rnams kyis yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid/brtson zhing nan tan byed pa/chos rnams kyi mtshan nyi rnam par 'byed pa la shing du mkhas par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/chos rnams kyi nges 
9b5(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i don la shin du shes par bya ba dang /chos rnams kyi don shin du shes par bya ba ba dang /chos rnams kyis rgyu dang /rkyen rnam pa sna tshogs shin du shes par bya ba dag/chos rnams kyi yang dag pa'i sgor 'jug par bya ba dang bzhi'o//
9b6(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang/don rnam pa bzhi mthong pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che na po rnams chos rnams kyi yang dag pas mtshan nyid la brtson zhing nan tan byed do//bzhi gang zhe na/tshad med pa'i chos kyi mtshan nyid shin du shes par bya ba 
9b7(mdo sna tshogs): 'byed pa la shin du mkhas par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/tshad mad pa'i chos kyi mtshan nyid/shin du shes par bya ba dang/tshad med pa'i nges pa'i chos bsgom pa la shin du mkhas pa bya ba dang /tshad med pa'i yon tan dang spyod 
9b8(mdo sna tshogs): pa 'phel zhing skye bar bya ba dang /chos rnamasakyi skye ba dang 'gag pa'i mtshan nyid shes pa dang mthong bar bya ba dang bzhi'o//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang don rnam pa bzhi mthong ba'i byang chub sems dpa' se mas dpa' 
10a1(mdo sna tshogs): chen po rnams chos rnams kyi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid la brtson zhing nan tan byed pa dang /chos rnams kyi mtshan nyid rnam par 'byed pa la shin du mkhas par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/bla na med pa yang 
10a2(mdo sna tshogs): dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la bsnyen par bya ba dang /byang chub kyi tshogs kyi chos rnams myur du yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /chos rnams kyi thabs la shin du mkhas pas/gzhan gyi drin la mi 'jog par bya ba dang /ye shes 
10a3(mdo sna tshogs): dang shes rab thams cad la shin du mkhas par bya ba'o//'jig rten 'dzin 'di dag ni don rnam pa bzhi mthong ba'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams chos rnams kyis yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid brtson zhing nan 
10a4(mdo sna tshogs): tan byed pa dang /chos rnam kyi mtshan nyid rnam par byed pa la shin du mkhas pa yin no//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang chos rnam pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' de sems dpa' chen po rnam chos rnams kyi 
10a5(mdo sna tshogs): yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid la brtson zhing nan tan byed pa dang /chos rna mas kyi mtshan nyid rnam par 'byed pa la shin du mkhas bar 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/sems can la phan pa dang bde ba'i phyir sems la ser sna'i dri ma med 
10a6(mdo sna tshogs): pa dang /rtag tu tshul khrims rnam par dag pa dang /brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la bde bar gnas pas brtson 'grus spyod pa la bar chad med pa dang /yang dag par yid la byed pas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la/shin 
10a7(mdo sna tshogs): tu nan tan byed pa dang bzhi'o//'jig rten 'dzin gzha' yang chos rnam pa bzhi dag ldan pa'i byang chub sems/dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams chos rnams kyi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid brtson zhing nan tan byed 
10a8(mdo sna tshogs): pa dang /chos rnams kyi mtshan nyid rnam pa bzhi dag pa'i mtshan nyid rnam par 'gyed pa la shin du mkhas par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhes na/smon lam phun sum tshogs pa shin tu dag pa dang ldan pa dang /spyod pa'i nan tan phun sum tshogs pa rnam par dag pa dang ldan pa dang
10b1(mdo sna tshogs): ji mi snyam pa'i bzod pa'i yon tan la bde bar gnas pa dang /chos rnams yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid rnam par 'byed pa'i 'od zer thob pa dang bzhi'o//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang chos rnam pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' 
10b2(mdo sna tshogs): sems dpa' chen po rnams chos rnams kyi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid la brtson zhing nan tan byed pa dang /chos rnams kyi mtshan nyid rnam par 'byedapa la shin du mkhas par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/mos pa chen pos thams 
10b3(mdo sna tshogs): cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes tshol ba dang /bsam gtan dang /rnam par grol ba dang /ting nge 'dzin rnam par 'byed pa la shin du mkhas pas mos pa chen po bskyed pa dang /byams pa dang /snying rje dang /dga' ba dang /btang snyoms 
10b4(mdo sna tshogs): chen po'i sems thob par bya ba'i phyir rnam par dag pa'i spyad pa spyod pa la sbyor ba dang /nges pa'i don la shin du legs par bsgom pa dang bzhi'o//'jig rte 'dzin gzhan yang chos rna ba bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang che ba sems dpa' 
10b5(mdo sna tshogs): sems dba' chen po rnams chos rnam kyi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyi la brtson zhing nan tan bye ba dang /chos rnams kyi mtshon nyid rnam par 'byed pa la shi na du mkhas par 'gyur ro //bzhi gang zhe na/shes rab kyi spyod yul 
10b6(mdo sna tshogs): phun sum tshogs pa dang /ye shes kyi spyod yul rnam par dag pa tsho la tshol ba dang /thogs pa med pa'i ye shes la mos pa dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi smon lam rtag tu mi spong ba'o//'jig rten 'dzin chos rnam 
10b7(mdo sna tshogs): pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams chos rnams kyi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid la brtson zhing nan tan byas pa dang /chos rnams kyi mtshan nyid rnam par 'byed pal shin du mkhas pa yin no//
10b8(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang don rnam pa bzhi mthong ba'i byang chub sems dpa' de sems dpa' chen po rnam par dran pa'i mthu tshol bar 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/dran pa'i dbang po yongs su rdzogs pa goms par bsgom par 
11a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//bya ba dang /shin du bde ba'i shes rab spyod pa dang /dran pa rgyun mi 'chad pa dang ldan pa dang /dran pa nye bar bzhag pa bzhi rdzogs par bsgom pa dang bzhi'o//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang don dam pa bzhi mthong ba'i byang 
11a2(mdo sna tshogs): chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams dran pa'i mthu tshol bar 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir rtag tu dran pa sems la 'jog pa dang /dran pa'i dbang po rno bas sngon gyi gnas na 
11a3(mdo sna tshogs): bsgom pa shes pas shes rab rnam par dag pa dang ldan pa dang /drang pa rgyun mi 'chad par myur du 'thob par bya ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi rgyu bskyed pa dang bzhi'o//'jig rte 'dzin don rnam pa bzhi mthong ba'i byang chub 
11a4(mdo sna tshogs): sems dpa' sems dpa' che na po rnams dran pa'i mthun tshol bar 'gyur ro//ba zhig gang zhe na/bsam pa'i thabs yongs su rdzogs pa sgom pa dang /yang dag pa'i ye shes dang shes rab sgom pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos thob par 
11a5(mdo sna tshogs): bya ba'i phyir shin du brtson 'grus bskyed pa dang /dran pa'i stobs kyi rgyun mi 'chad pa thob par bya ba'i phyir dran pa mi brjed pa'o//'jig rten 'dzin 'di dag ni don rnam pa bzhi mthong pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
11a6(mdo sna tshogs): chen po rnams dran pa'i mthu tshol ba yin no//'jig rten 'dzin chos rnam pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i sems dpa' chen po rnams dran pa'i mthuthob par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/shes rab shin du byang 
11a7(mdo sna tshogs): pa dran pa'i rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad pa'i brtson 'grus dang /chos rnams kyi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid thob par bya ba'i phyir rtse gcig pa'i sems kyis rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad pa dang /chos thams cad yang dag par dran par bya ba'i 
11a8(mdo sna tshogs): phyir rtag tu le lo med pa dang /yang dag pa'i yid la bya ba'i phyir rtag tu dbang po rnams bsrung ba dang bzhi'o//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang chos rnam pa bzhi dang ldanapa'i byang chub sems dpa' chen po 
11b1(mdo sna tshogs): rnams/dran pa'i mthu thob par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/tshul khrims rnam par dag pa la gnas pa dang /spyod lam rnam par dag pa dang ldan pa dang /spyod pa'i gnas kun du sems kyi sgrib pa lnga yongs su bsal te 'jig rten 
11b2(mdo sna tshogs): gyi chos la mi chags pa dang /las kyi sgrib pa dang nyon mongs pa'i sgrib pa yongs su spangs pa dang bzhi'o//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang chos rnam pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams dran 
11b3(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i mthu thob par 'gyur ro ro//bzhi gang zhe na/mi 'khrugs pa'i sems kyis dge ba'i chos tshol ba dang /sems kyi mtshan nyid gcig la goms par bsgom pa dang /chos rnams kyi rnam grangs la yang dag par 'jug cing 
11b4(mdo sna tshogs): shin du mkhas pa dang /'du 'dzi la mi mos pas khyim na gnas pa dang rab tu byung ba'i gang zag spong ba dang bzhi'o//'jig rten gyi 'dzin gzhan yang chos rnams pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byangs chub sems dpa' sems pa dpa' chen po 
11b5(mdo sna tshogs): rnams dran pa'i mthu thob par 'gyur ba'o//bzhi gang zhe na/dge ba'i bshes gnyen la bsten pa dang /rtag tu chos zab mo la nan tan byed pa dang /rtag tu sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa'i gan du 'gro ba la dga' 
11b6(mdo sna tshogs): ba dang /gsol ba dang zhu ba la dga' zhing shes rab sgom pa la mos pa'o//'di dag ni chos rnam pa 'di bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams dran pa'i mthu thob ba yin no//'jig rten 
11b7(mdo sna tshogs): 'dzin gzhan yang chos rnam pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chung sems dpa' sems dpa'achen po rnams chos thams cad kyi rnam grangs rnam par 'byed pa la mkhas shing shes rab la nan tan byed par 'gyur ro//bzhi 
11b8(mdo sna tshogs): gang zhe na/chos thams cad kyi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid la shin tu mkhas pa dang /chos thams cad kyi rgyur gyur pa rnam par 'byed pa la shin tu mkhas pa dang chos thams cad kyi nges pa'i don la mkhas pa dang 
12a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//
12b1(mdo sna tshogs): thabs la 'jug pa dang bzhi'o//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang chos rnam pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams chos thams cad kyi rnam grangs rnam par 'byed pa la mkhas shing shes rab la 
12b2(mdo sna tshogs): nan tan byed par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/sdug bsngal ba'i chos thams cad la shin du mkhas pa dang /kun 'byung gi chos thams cad la shin tu mkhas pa dang /'gog pa'i chos thams cad la shin tum mkhas pa dang /
12b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'gog pa dang lam gyi chos thams cad la shin mkhas pa dang bzhi'o//'jig rte 'dzin gzhan yang chos rna pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i bya chub sems dpa' sems pa dang chen po rnams chos thams  cad kyi rnam grangs rnam
12b4(mdo sna tshogs): par 'byed pa la mkhas shingashes rab la nan tan byed par 'gyur /ro//bzhi gang zhe na/chos rnams 'du ba dang 'jig pa'i sbyor ba la mkhas pa dang /sngon gyi rgyus mthu thob pa dang /chos thams cad kyi ci dang cis 'dul ba la
12b5(mdo sna tshogs): shin tu mkhas pa dang /yi ge dang /'bru dang /tshig rnam par 'byed pa la shin tu mkhas pa dang bzhi'o//jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang chos rnam pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams
12b6(mdo sna tshogs): chos thams cad kyi rnam grangs rnam par 'byed pa la mkhas shi shes rab la nan tan byedapar 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/bkri ba'i don gyi mdo sde la shin tu mkhas pa dang/nges pa'i don gyi mdo sde'i tshig gi rjes su mi
12b7(mdo sna tshogs): 'brang ba dang /chos thams cad kye mtshan nyid kyi rgyu la shin tu mkhas pa dang /chos thams cad kyi mtshan ma med pa'i ye shes la bde bar gnas pa ste/'jig rten 'dzin 'di dag ni chos rnam pa bzhi dang ldang pa'i byang chub sems
12b8(mdo sna tshogs): dpa' sems dpa' chen pornams chos thams cad kyi rnam grags rnam par 'byed pa la la mkhas shing shes rab la nan tan bye pa yin no//'jig rten 'dzin chos bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang cub sems dpa' sems dpa
13a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//chen po rnams lus 'di bor nas bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi bar du rgyun mi 'chad pa'i dran pa dang ldan par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/dge ba dang mi dge ba'i chos la shin du rtogs pa 
13a2(mdo sna tshogs): dang /dran pa dang /shes rab phul du gyur pa dang /sems kyi sgrib pa lnga yongs su spangs pa dang /nam du yang bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi dran pa mi nyams pa dang bzhi'o//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang chos 
13a3(mdo sna tshogs): rnam pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams lus 'di bor? nas nam bla na med pa yang dag pas rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi ba du rgyun mi 'chad pa'i dran? pa dang ldan par 'gyur ? ro//bzhi gang 
13a4(mdo sna tshogs): zhe na/dran pa nye bar bzhag pa bzhi la shin du nan tan byed pa dang /rnam par 'byed pa'i shes rab la shin du nan tan byed ba dang /ting nge 'dzin rnams kyi thog mar shes rab gtong ba dang /nges pa'i shes rab khong du chud pa 
13a5(mdo sna tshogs): dang bzhi'o//'jig rten 'jig gzhan yang chos rnam pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byung chub sems dpa' sems pa'i chen po rnams lus 'di bar nas nam bla na med pa dang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi byang du rgyun mi 
13a6(mdo sna tshogs): 'chad pa'i dran pa dang ldan par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zhe na/gzungs kyi sgo thob pa dang /ma skyes pa'i ye shes sgom pa dang /zad pa'i ye shes la 'jug pa dang /'gog pa'i ye shes rtogs par bya ba dang bzhi'o//'jig rten 
13a7(mdo sna tshogs): 'dzin gzhan yang chos rnam pa bzh-i dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams lus 'di bor nas nam bla na med par yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byangs chub kyi bar du rgyun mi 'chad pa'i dran pa dang ldan
13a8(mdo sna tshogs): par 'gyur ro//bzhi gang zha na/byams sdang med par bya ba dang /'du byas kyi chos thams cad la mi chags pa dang /'du ma byas pa'i ye shes rtogs pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul du phyin par byed pa'o//
13b1(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten 'dzin 'di dag ni chos rnam pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams lus 'di bor nas nam bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi bar du rgyun mi 'chad pa'i dran pa dang ldan pa 
13b2(mdo sna tshogs): yin no//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang chos rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs rnam pa lnga dang ldan pa'i byang chub sem dpa' sems dpa' chen pa rnams yon tan gong ma rnams rdzogs par 'gyur ro//lnga gang zhe na/
13b3(mdo sna tshogs): shin du lhag pa'i bsam pa rnam par dag pa'i ye shesakyi stobs dang /smon lam rnam par dag pa'i ye shesakri stobs dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam par dag pa'i ye she kyi stobs dang /bsngo ba rnam par dag pa'i yeshes kyi 
13b4(mdo sna tshogs): stobs dang /las kyi sgrib pa rnam par dag pa'i yeshe kyi stobs dang lnga'o//'jig rten 'dzin gzhanayang rnam par dag pa'i ye shese kyi stobs rnam pa lnga dang dang ldan pa'i byang chub su sems dpa' sems dpa'achen po rnams 
13b5(mdo sna tshogs): yon tan gong ma rnams rdzongs par 'gyur ro//lnga gad zhe na/spyod lam gyi gnas rnam par dag pa'i ye shais kyi stobs dang /dran pa dang ldan pa rnam par dag pa'i ye+ai shes kyi stobs dang /sbyor ba'i rnam par dag pa'i ye
13b6(mdo sna tshogs): shes kyi stobs dang /sems la dmigs pa'i rnam par dag pa'i ye shed kyi stobs dang /mtshan nyid la dmigs pa'i rnam par thag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs dang lnga'o//'jog rten 'dzin gzhan yang rnam par dag pa'i yeshais
13b7(mdo sna tshogs): kyi stobs rnam pa lnga dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams yon tan gong ma rnams rdzogs par 'gyur ro//lnga gang zhe na/gtong ba'i sems rnam par dag pa'i yeshes kyi stobs dang /sem can la 
13b8(mdo sna tshogs): phan pa dang bde ba rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs dag byams pa chen po bskyed pa'i rnam par dag pa'i yi shes kyi stobs dang /snying rje chen po bskyed pa'i rnam pa dag pa'i ye shes kyi stob dang /dga' ba 
14a1(mdo sna tshogs): chen po dang /btang snyoms chen po bskyed pa'i rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs dang lnga'o//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang rnam par dag pa'i ye shesakyi stobs rnam pa lnga dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' 
14a2(mdo sna tshogs): sems dpa' chen po rnams yon tan gong ma rnams rdzogs par 'gyur ro//lnga gang zhe na/tshul khrims bsrungs ba'i rnam par dag pa'i ye she kyi stobs dang /tshul khrims bsrungs pa la ma chags pa'i rnam par dag pa'i
14a3(mdo sna tshogs): me shes kyi stobs dang /bzod pa rnam par dag pa'i ye shed kyi stobs dang /bzodapa la ma chags pa rnam par dag pa'i ye shos kyi stobs dag mang du thos pa rnam par dag pa'i ya shes kyi stobs dang lnga'o/'jig rten 
14a4(mdo sna tshogs): 'dzin gzhan yang rnam par bar dag pa'i ye shes kyi sto bas rnam pa lnga dang ldan ba'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnam yon tan gong ma rnams rdzegs par 'gyur ro//lda gang zhe na/lhag pa'i brtson 'grus rnam 
14a5(mdo sna tshogs): par dag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs dang /brtson 'grus len pa rnam par dag pa'i yeshes kyi stobs dang /bsam gtan rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyi stebs dang /bsam gtan gyi thabs rnam par dag pa'i ye shes
14a6(mdo sna tshogs): kyi stobs dang /zhi gnas dang lhag mthong gi thabs rnam par dag pa'i yi shes kyi stobs dang lnga'o/'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang rnam paradag pa'i ye shes kyi stobasarnam pa lnga dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' 
14a7(mdo sna tshogs): sems dpa' chen po rnams yon tan gong ma rnams/rdzogs par 'gyur ro//lnga gang zhe na/shes rab rnam par dag pa'i ye she kyi stobs dang /mang du thos pa'i nges par sbyor ba rnam par dag pa'i ye shos kyi stobs dang /
14a8(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'da' ba rnam par dag pa'i yeshos kyi stobs dang /shes rab dang /thabs rnam par dag pa'i ya shes kyi stobs dang /'du byas dang 'dus ma byas rnam par dag pa'i ye shis kyi stobs 
14b1(mdo sna tshogs): dang lnga'o//'jig rten 'dzin gzhan yang rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs rnam pa lnga dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams yon tan gong ma rnams rdzogs par 'gyur ro//lnga gang 
14b2(mdo sna tshogs): zhe na/thabs la rtog pa rnam par dag pa'i ye shesakyi stobs dang /rig pa dang /rnam par grol ba rnam par dag pa'i ye shes:kyi stobs dang /ma skyes pa'i mtshan nyid rnam par dag pa'i ye shes
14b3(mdo sna tshogs): pa dang mtshan nyid med pa rnam par dag pa'i ye shis kyi stobs dang /don dam pa dang kun rdzob rnam par dag pa'i ye shas kyi stobs so//'jig rten 'dzin de dag ni rnam par dag pa'i ye she kyi stobs rnam pa lnga dang dan pa'i 
14b4(mdo sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpa' sem sa dpa' chen po rnams myur du yon tan gong ma rnams rjogs pa yin no//'jig rten 'dzin de bas na byang chub sems dpa' semams dpa' chen po rnams rnam par dag pa'i ye sher kyi stobs de dag
14b5(mdo sna tshogs): bsgom pa la shin du brtson par bya'o//'jig rten 'dzin chos rnam pa gsum dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams rnal par dag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs la brtson par 'gyur ro//gsum gang zhe na/
14b6(mdo sna tshogs): mos pa dang /brtson 'grus dang /bag byed pa'o//chos rnam pa 'di gsum dang ldan pa'i byang 'chub sems dpa' sems dbang chen po rnams yon tan thams cad rdzogs pa'i rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyis stobs la brtson
14b7(mdo sna tshogs): pa yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'jig rten 'dzin mos pa dang /brtson 'grul dang /bag byed pa ni chos thams cad kyi rtsa ba yin pas byang chub sems dpa' seMms dpa' chen po rnams rna ma par dag pa'i ye shes gyi sdobs
14b8(mdo sna tshogs): la nan tan byed pa ni myur du thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes thob par 'gyur te/brtson 'grus las phyir mi ldog pa zhes bya'o//chos las phyir mi ldog pa zhe bya'o//yon tan de nyid myur du 'phel zhing skye ba'am 'thob par
15a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//'gyur la/chos thams cad la rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs kyang myur du 'thob bo//'jig rten 'dzin gang zag gang zhig de ltar chos thams cad la rnam par/dag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs/thob pa ni 
15a2(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten gyi bsod nams kyi zhing zhes bya'o//gang zag de ni nga'i 'og tu mchod pa'i rnyed pa zhu ba zhes bya'o//gang zag de na de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul gyi gnas su phyin pa zhes bya'o//gang zag de de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
15a3(mdo sna tshogs): chos rtogs pa zhes bya'o//gang zag nges ring po mi thogs par de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes chud par 'gyur ro//'jig rten 'dzin nga sngon bskal pa tshad med grangs med pa'i bar du byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa na/
15a4(mdo sna tshogs): sangs rgyas mar me mdzad kyis nga la khyod bskal pa grangs med pa 'das nas mngon par 'tshang rgya'o zhes lung bstan te/ngas de'i tshe nyid na rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyis stobs de dag kun shes par gyur to//'jig rten 'dzin 
15a5(mdo sna tshogs): gang zag gang zhig chos thams cad la de lta bu'i rnam par dag pa'i ye shesakyi stobs dang ldan par 'gyur ba ni/gang zag des bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub thob pa na da ltar ngas ji ltar thob pa bzhin no//gang 
15a6(mdo sna tshogs): zag des chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bar 'gyur ba'ang des ji ltar bskor ba bzhin no//gang zag des seng ge'i sgra sgrog par 'gyur ba'ang ji ltar sgra sgrogs pa bzhin no//gang zag nges rang bzhin gyis chos thams cad la 
15a7(mdo sna tshogs): dbang po dang stobs thob pa yang da ltar gyi ji lta ba bzhin no//'jig rten 'dzin khyod kyis kyang rnam par dag pa'i ye sheskyi stobs 'di bsgom pa la brtson par byas na ring por mi thogs par ye shes dang shes rab thams 
15a8(mdo sna tshogs): cad lhun gyis grub par 'gyur ro//'jig rten 'dzin sngon bskal pa grangs med tshad med pa 'das pa de'i tshe de'i dus na/bcom ldan 'das ye shes mtho ba'i rgyal po zhes bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa mkhyen pa dang zhabs 
15b1(mdo sna tshogs): su ldan pa/bde bar gshegs pa/'jig rten mkhyen pa/bla na med pa skyes bu 'dul zhing kha lo bsgyur ba/lha dang mi'i ston pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten du byung ngo //'jig rten 'dzin bcom ldan 'das ye shes 
15b2(mdo sna tshogs): mtho ba'i rgyal po de la nyan thos kyi dge 'dun tshad med pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun chad med pa yod do//de yang bcom ldan 'das de'i sngon gyi smon lam gyi rgyu dang rkyen gyi dbang gis so//bcom ldan 'das ye shes 
15b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'tho ba'i rgyal po'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing de na ngan song gsum gyi ming yang mi grag ste/sems can de dag kyang sdug bsngal yod par mi tshor zhing mthar bde ba dang skyid pa phun sum tshogs pa dang ldan pas 'dod pa yongs su 
15b4(mdo sna tshogs): spangs pa mang ste/sgrib pa lnga yang rab tu bcil ba sha stag go/sems can de dag de ltar yongs su dag pa'i bde ba dang ldan pa ni bsam gtan bzhi pa'i bde ba thob pa bzhin no//bcom ldan 'das ye she mtho ba'i rgyal po 
15b5(mdo sna tshogs): de'i tshe'i tshad ni bskal pa khri phrag khrag khrig snyed do//de'i tshe na 'jig rten de'i rgyal po yang de bzhin gshegs pas mdzad de/de ma yin pa'i rgyal po gzhan med pas yul de'i sems can dag bcom ldan 'das la chos kyi 
15b6(mdo sna tshogs): rgyal po zhes brjod do//bcom ldan 'das ye she/mtho ba'i rgyal po de byang chub sems dpa' rnams la sems can thams cad kyi the tsom gcod cing sems can thams cad kyis sems mgu bar 'gyur ba zhes bya ba'i byang chub 
15b7(mdo sna tshogs): sems dpa'i sde snod kyi mdo ston to//de'i tshe na byang chub sems dpa' lnga brgyas byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs bshad pa thos nas 'di lta bu'i brtson 'grus kyi stobs bskyed par gyur to//
15b8(mdo sna tshogs): 'di lta ste/nam mtsho'i bar du 'dug pa'i sems mi bskyed pa dang /nam mtsho'i bar du gos su 'du shes pa mi bskyed pa dang /nam 'tsho'i bar du bdag tu 'du shes pa dang /sems can du 'du shes pa dang /skyes pa dang /
16a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//bud med du 'du shes pa mi bskyed pa dang /nam mtsho'i bar du zas mang du mi za zhing 'di lta bu'i rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs la nan tan byed do//brtson 'grus la rab tu 'bar ba'i byang chub sems dpa' lnga 
16a2(mdo sna tshogs): brgya po de dag dge ba'i rtsa ba de nyid kyi rgyu dang rkyen gyis tshe 'phos nas thams cad shar phyogs su sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di nas khri phrag bcu 'das pa'i pha rol gyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing du skyes so//der skyes nas chos de la nan tan 
16a3(mdo sna tshogs): byas pas ring po mi thogs par sngon gyi gnas dran te dbang po rnon por gyur to//sangs rgyas kyi zhing de na chad med pa'i me tog brtsegs pa'i rgyal pa zhes bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa da ltar bzhugs te/'tsho skyong zhing chos 
16a4(mdo sna tshogs): ston to//byang chub sems dpa' de dag lo bcu drug lon nas/bcom ldan 'das tshad med pa'i me tog brtsegasapa'i rgyal po'i bstan pa la rab tu byung ste/lo khri phrag drug bcu'i bar du gzhon nur tshangs par spyad pa spyod cing 
16a5(mdo sna tshogs): de lta bu'i brtson 'grus dang nan tan byed do//'jig rten 'dzin byang chub sems dpa' lnga brgya po de dag da ltar de bzhin gshegs pa khri phrag nyi shu dang phrad de/bcom ldan 'das de dag gi spyan sngar brtson 'grus shin du brtsam 
16a6(mdo sna tshogs): pas dran pa dang /bde ba dang /shes rab phul du gyur pa dang ldan par gyur to//tha ma bcom ldan 'das tshad med ba stobs mtho ba'i rgyal po zhes bya ba dang phrad nas bcom ldan 'das des lung bstan pa mdo bskal pa khri 'das nas 
16a7(mdo sna tshogs): bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya'o zhes lung bstan to//byang chub sems pa'i lnga brgya po de dag kyang bskal pa khri'i bar du sangs rgyas stong phrag gnyis dang phrad nas sangs rgyas kyi byang 
16a8(mdo sna tshogs): chub yongs su rdzogs te/bskal pa gcig la bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu ma ngon par sangs rgyas so//'jig rten 'dzin de bas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams myur du bla na med pa yang 
16b1(mdo sna tshogs): dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub thob par 'dod pa dag rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyi stobs la de ltar mos pa dang /brtson 'grus dang /bag byed pa bskyed par rig par bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'jig rten 'dzin sangs rgyas rnams kyi bla na 
16b2(mdo sna tshogs): med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chung thob pa'i rtsa ba ni mos pa dang /brtson 'gru dang /bag byed pa dang /gzhan yang byang chub kyi tshogs su gyur pa'i chos gang sangs rgyas kyi chos phun sum tshogs par byed pa rnams so//'jig rten 
16b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'dzin sa de lta bu'i brtson 'grus kyis sangs rgyas khri phrag nyi shu dang phrad de/sangs rgyas de dag gi chos kyang skye ba'i gnas thams cad du dran pa dang ldan par 'gyur/skye ba'i gnas thams cad tu sngon gyi gnas dran pas chos 
16b4(mdo sna tshogs): 'di bsgom pa la bar chad med de/ngas mos pa dang /brtson 'gyus bag byas pa las ma mnyams pas rtag tu mos pa dang /brtson 'gru'i dang /bag yod pa dang ldan par gyur to//de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das thugs rje dang byams pa chen 
16b5(mdo sna tshogs): po'i thugs kyis phyogs bzhir gzigs nas mngon par shes pa'i rdzu 'phrul gyi mthu bstan te/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi 'dzam bu'i gling kun tu sprul pa'i sangs rgyas dag bzhugs shing sems 
16b6(mdo sna tshogs): can rnams la sems can thams cad kyi the tshom gcod cing /sems can thams cad kyi sems mgu bar byed pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod ces bya ba'i mdo ston par gyur to//gzhan yang rdzu 'phrul gyi mthus 'od 
16b7(mdo sna tshogs): ma'i tshal gyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor kun kyang sprul pa'i sangs rgyas kyis 'dzam bu'i gling gang ba bzhugs shing so so nas dam pa'i chos ston par mthong nas/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rnams rab tu dga' bar 'gyur te/
16b8(mdo sna tshogs): 'dug pa las langs nas sangs rgyas la phyag 'tshal te 'di skad du ngo mtshar che'o//bcom ldan 'das sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi rdzu 'phrul gyi mthu bsam gyis mi khyab bo//tshad med pa'i bsam gyis mi khyab 
17a1(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i chos lhun gyis grub po zhes smras so//de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis 'khor mang po rnams la bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu dag de bzhin gshegs pas bstan pa'i dngos po 'di dag ngo mtshar che ba ma 
17a2(mdo sna tshogs): yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de bzhin gshegs pa chos nyid thugs su chud pas pa spu'i khung bu gcig nas kyang mngon par shes pa'i rdzu 'phrul gyi mthus 'od zer bkye nas phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams gang gA'i klung gi bye 
17a3(mdo sna tshogs): ma snyed snang bar mdzad cing chos kyi gsung sgrogs kyang ba spu'i khung bu gcig nas/bstan pa brgya stong gi cha gcig tsam yang rdzogs pa ma yin te/de bzhin gshegs pa ni de ltar bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i yon tan lhun gyis 
17a4(mdo sna tshogs): grub pa'o//rigs kyi bu dag de bzhin bshegs pa ni sems can de dag gi bsam pa thugs su chud pas de la chos ston to//rigs kyi bu rnams da ltar gyi dus kyi sems can de dag ni chos 'di spyod pa la mos pa nyung ngo //
17a5(mdo sna tshogs): rigs kyi bu dag da ltar gyi dus kyi sems pa can dag ni chos 'di spyod pa brtson 'grus rtsom pa nyung ngo //rigs kyi bu dag da ltar kyi dus kyi sems can dag ni chos 'di la bag byed par spyod pa'ang nyung ngo //de ci'i phyir zhe na/dus ngan 
17a6(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i sems can dag ni chos 'ad la spyod pa'ang rab tu nyung ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa ni rnyog pa lnga'i 'jig rten ngan par 'byung ngo //'di lta ste/sems can gyi rnyog pa dang /lta ba'i rnyog pa dang /tshe'i rnyog pa dang /nyon mongs pa'i

23a4(mdo sna tshogs): yang mthong ngo //ri dang /chu dang /nags tshal la sogs par mthong ste/de ltar rmi lam gyis gzugs mtshan ma de la nges par chad pa med de rnam pa btags pa'i 'du shes las gzugs kyi phung po'i mtshan mar skyed pa yang 
23a5(mdo sna tshogs): de bzhin te/sngon gyi las kyi rgyu dang rkyen las skyes te nges pa'i rang bzhin can ma yin no//byang chub sems dpa' rnams de ltar yid la byed pa na gzugs la bdag gam bdag gi bar mi len no//yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du 
23a6(mdo sna tshogs): gzugs la rtog ste/yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin/du mi rtag pa'i mtshan nyid dang /yang dag pa ma yin pa phyin ci log tu rtog go/sems can dag ni phyin ci log gis bslad pas gzugs la bdag gzugs/bdag gi gzugs 
23a7(mdo sna tshogs): gzhan gzugs/gzhan gyi gzugs su zhen cing 'dzin to//de ltar gzugs rnam par 'byed pa la rtog pa'i tshe gzugs su mi dmigs/gzugs kyi rang bzhin mi mthong /gzugs mi rtag pa la yang mi chags so//
23a8(mdo sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpas de'i tshe gzugs la chags pa dang 'dzin pa thams cad bsal te med par gyur na gzugs kyi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid la shin du mkhas pa yin/gzugs mnyam pa'i mtshan nyid shes pa yin/
23b1(mdo sna tshogs): gzugs 'gog pa'i mtshan nyid shes pa yin/gzugs 'gog pa'i lam gyi mtshan nyid la mkhas pa yin/gzugs kyi phung po gang nas kyang ma 'ongs pa gang du yang mi 'gro bar rab tu rtogs so//de ltar rtog pa'i tshe na lus 
23b2(mdo sna tshogs): dang gzugs kyi phung po de las kyi 'bras bu rnam par smin pa dang /rnam par brtags pa las skyes ste/chen po bzhi 'dus pa las byung bar shes so//lus kyi gzugs kyi phung po ni bdag ma yin/gzhan ma yin/bdag po med 
23b3(mdo sna tshogs): de/gang nas kyang ma 'ongs so//gzugs kyi phung po la de ltar rtog pas nang gi gzugs la mi chags mi len/phyi'i gzugs la mi chags mi lan/'das pa'i gzugs la mi chags ma lan/ma 'ongs pa'i gzugs la mi chag
23b4(mdo sna tshogs): mi len/da lta'i gzugs la yang mi chags mi len/gzugs kyi phung po thams cad la ma skyes pa'i mtshan nyid du shes so//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' de'i tshe gzugs mi 'gag gzugs 'gag pa'i chos tshol lo//
23b5(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten 'dzin de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams gzugs rnam par 'byed pa la rtog pa zhes bya'o//'jig rten 'dzin de la ji ltar na byang chub sems pa'i sems dpa' chen po rnam tshor ba nye 
23b6(mdo sna tshogs): bar len pa'i phung po rnam par 'byed pa la rtogs pa yin zhe na/byang chub sems dpas 'di snyam du sems te/bde bar tshor ba dang /sdug bsngal du tshor ba dang /bde ba yang ma yin sdug bsngal yang ma yin par tshor ba 'di ni rten 
23b7(mdo sna tshogs): cing 'brel ba las byung ste/rgyu dang rkyen la rag lus pas tshor ba'i mtshan nyid la zhugs na/de la tshor bar byed pa med de zhen pa tsam du zad do//zhen pa de ni yang dag pa ma yin te/yang dag pa ma yin pa'i kun du brtags pa 
23b8(mdo sna tshogs): las skyes pa'o//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' de de snyam du sems pa na yang 'di snyam du/bus ba so so'i skye bo ni yang dag pa ma yin pa'i kung du rtog pa'i tshor bas bcings nas tshor ba rnam 
24a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//pa gsum gyis rnam par gtses so//'di lta ste/bde ba dang /sdug bsngal dang /bde ba yang ma yin sdug bsngal yang ma yin pa'i tshor ba rnams so//byis pa so so'i skye bo dag gal te bde bar tshor ba na 'dod pa'i 
24a2(mdo sna tshogs): nyon mongs pas bkol te/'dod pa'i nye ba'i nyon mongs pas nyon mongs pa'i phyir mi dge ba'i las rnam pa sna tshogs bskyed do//gal te sdug bsngal du tshor ba na zhe sdang gi nyon mongs pas bkol te/zhe sdang gi nye ba'i nyon 
24a3(mdo sna tshogs): mongs pas nyon mongs pa'i phyer mi dge ba'i las rnam par sna tshogs bskyed do//galas te bde ba yang ma yin sdug pa sngal yang ma yin par tshor ba na ma rig pa'i nyon mongs pas bkol te ma rig pa'i nye ba'i nyon mongs 
24a4(mdo sna tshogs): pas nyon mongs pa'i phyir slos pa'i mya ngan dang /smre sngags 'don pa dang /sdug bsngal gyis gdungs pa las rnam par ma grol bar gyur to//bdag gis byis pa so so'i skye bo'i rjes su bslab par mi bya'i chos rnams kyi yang 
24a5(mdo sna tshogs): dag pa nyid la brtag par bya'o//bdag cag gis yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin tshor ba la brtag par bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' rnams yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhIn tshor ba'i phung po la brtags nas yang 'di snyam du phung po ma 
24a6(mdo sna tshogs): yin pa ni tshor ba'i phung po ste/rnam par brtags pa las skyes te/phyin ci log dang ldan pas tshor ba byed pa med de/sngon gyi las kyi rgyu las skyes so//da ltar gyi rkyen gyi tshor ba rnams myong ste/rang bzhin gyis stong pas tshor 
24a7(mdo sna tshogs): ba la tshor ba'i mtshan ma med do//byang chub sems dpa'-i rnams tshor ba'i phung po la rtog pa na chu'i chu bur lta bur skyes nas 'gags te nges par chad pa med par chud pa bzhin tshor ba'i phung po yang de dang 'dra ste/rgyu dang 
24a8(mdo sna tshogs): rkyen 'brel ba las skyes te rgyu dang rkyen la rag las pas skad cig kyang mi gnas te/yang dag pa ma yin pas rnam par brtags pa'i phyin ci log las skyes so//byang chub sems dpa'ang dag de'i tshe na yang 'di snyam du 
24b1(mdo sna tshogs): byis pa so so'i skye bo dag ni snying brtse ba'i gnas yin no//tshor ba'i phung po la yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin mi rtogs pas tshor ba rnams kyis nyon mongs par gyur ro//bde ba'i tshor ba la yang chags/sdug bsngal gyi tshor ba la yang 
24b2(mdo sna tshogs): chags/bde ba yang ma yin sdug bsngal yang ma yin pa'i tshor ba la yang chags pa bskyed de/tshor ba rnams kyis bcings pas 'khor ba na 'khor zhing lus gcig nas lus gcig tu rgyun mi 'chad de/tshor ba'i nyon mongs pas 
24b3(mdo sna tshogs): ni tshor ba'i phung po'i mtshan nyid yang dag pa ji lta ba mi shes pa'i phyir ro//bdag cag gis byis pa so so'i skye bo'i rjes su bslab par mi bya'i yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhi na tshor ba'i phung po rnam par 'byed pa la brtag par bya'o//
24b4(mdo sna tshogs): de'i tshe yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhi'i tshor ba'i phung po la rtog pa ni phung po med pa tshor ba'i phung po yin pa dang /yang dag pa ma yin pa'i phung po de tshor ba'i phung po yin pa pa dang /phyin ci log gi phung po de tshor ba'i phung pa yin pa dang /
24b5(mdo sna tshogs): mi gnas pa'i phung po de tshor ba'i phang po yin par rtogs te/de'i tshe na yang dag pa ji lta ba ba bzhin du tshor ba'i phung po'i mtshan nid mthong ste/byas pa med/byed pa med de/tshor ba'i phung po la tshor ba'i phung po'i mtshan 
24b6(mdo sna tshogs): nyid kyang mi mthong ngo //de ltar tshor ba'i phung po la rtogs pa na tshor ba'i phung po la nang na gnas par mi mthong /tshor ba'i phung po la phyi rol na gnas par mi mthong ste/tshor ba bdag ces bya ba la mi chags/tshor ba bdag gi bzhes bya ba la mi 
24b7(mdo sna tshogs): chags pas/tshor ba'i phung po la gang nas kyang ma 'ongs la bdag po med de chos gang gis kyang ma bskyed par shes so//tshor ba de ni phyin ci log dang ldan pa dang /sngon gyi las kyi 'bras bu rnam par smin pa la tshor ba'i phung po 
24b8(mdo sna tshogs): zhes rnam grangs su btags par shes so//tshor ba'i phung po la yang dag pa ma yin pa'i rgyu dang rkyen rgyun mi 'chad par spyod par mthong ste/de'i dus na 'das pa'i tshor ba'i phung po la mi len mi chags/mi zhe na/ma 'ongs pa'i

25b1(mdo sna tshogs): zhe sdang gi kun nas nyon mongs pa bsal ba'i phyir brtson 'grus rtsom mo//bde ba yang ma yin sdug bsngal yang ma yin pa'i tshor ba myong ba las ni gti mug gi kun nas nyon mongs ba bsal ba'i phyir brtson 'gru rtsom ste/
25b2(mdo sna tshogs): yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du tshor ba rnam pa gsum shes par byed do//de'i tshe na ji tsam du tshor bar 'gyur ba gal te bde bar tshor ba dang /sdug bsngal bar tshor ba dang /bde ba yang ma yin sdug sngal yang ma yin pa'i tshor
25b3(mdo sna tshogs): kun la'ang mi chags te ring du gyur na'ang 'dod chags kyi kun nas nyon mongs pa dang bral bar 'gyur/zhe sdang gi kun nas nyon mngos pa dang bral bar 'gyur /gti mug gi kun nas nyon mangs pa dang bral bar 'gyu
25b4(mdo sna tshogs): tshor ba 'byung ba'i dus na tshor ba'i phung po la yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhinami rtag pa dang /yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhi du mthong zhing shes par 'gyur ro//gal te de ltar shes nas tshor ba'i phung po la 'dod pa'i chags pa yongs
25b5(mdo sna tshogs): su gcod par 'gyur te/yongs su gcod pa'i tshor ba'i phung po la zhugs pas 'dod cing chags pa'i lam dag tu tshor ba rnams kyis nyon mongs par mi 'gyur ro//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis gal te de ltar
25b6(mdo sna tshogs): yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin tshor ba'i phung po rtogs shing yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhIn tshor ba'i phung po shes nas tshor ba'i phung pa'i kun 'byung dang /tshor ba'i phung po'i 'gog pa dang /tshor ba'i phung pa'i 'gog pa'i lam yongs
25b7(mdo sna tshogs): su shes par 'gyur ro//de nas tshor ba'i phung pos ma skyes pa'i mtshan nyid du shes/ma skyes pa'i mtshan nyid kyis tshor ba'i phung po la mtshan ma med pa khong du chud cing rtogs par 'gyur ro//'jig rten 'dzin 'di
25b8(mdo sna tshogs): dag ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams tshor ba'i phung po rnam par 'byed pa la rtog pa zhes bya'o//'jig rten 'dzin de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' ses dpa' chen po rnams 'du shes kyi
26a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//phung po rnam par 'byed pa la rtog ce na/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin 'du shes kyi phung po la rtog pa'i tshe 'du shes kyi phung po de phyin ci log las skyes te yang dag 
26a2(mdo sna tshogs): pa ma yin pa/snying po med pa/mi brtan pa/thog ma nas ma skyes pa'i mtshan nyid chad pas rgyu dang rkyen 'dus pa dang /sngon gyi las kyi mthu las byung ba mthong ngo //yang 'di snyam du phung po ma yin pa ni 'du shes kyi phung po ste/yang 
26a3(mdo sna tshogs): dag pa ma yin pa'i phung po ni 'du shes kyi phung po'o//phyin ci log gi phung po ni 'du shes kyi phur ba'o//'du shes kyi phung po la 'du shes kyi phung po'i mtshan ma med pa ni dper na dbyar gyi smig rgyu ba lta bu ming tsam ste/smig rgyu ba'i 
26a4(mdo sna tshogs): phung po dang 'dra bar 'du shes kyi phung po 'di yang de dang 'dra ste/rnam par shes pa'i 'du shes kyis 'du shes zhes btags so//byis pa so so'i skye bo ni yang dag pa ma yin pa 'du shes 'dis bcings te/bde bar rnam par shes 
26a5(mdo sna tshogs): sdug bsngal du rnam par shes/bde ba yang ma yin sdug bsngal yang ma yin par rnam par shes/grang ba dang tsha bar rnam par shes/skyes pa dang bud med du rnam par shes/lam rgyud lngar 'khor bar rnam par shes/
26a6(mdo sna tshogs): 'dus par rnam par shes/gyes par rnam par shes/'das par rnam par shes/ma 'ongs par rnam par shes/da ltar gyis rnam par shes/bzang por rnam par shes/ngan par rnam par shes/yod par rnam par shes/
26a7(mdo sna tshogs): med par rnam par shes so//byis pa so so'i skyes bo 'du shes ni phyin ci log yang dag pa ma yin pa'i dbang du gyur bas rgyu dang rkyen la rag lus te 'du shes kyi phung po zhes btags pa tsam du zad do//de la ni nang ngam phyi 
26a8(mdo sna tshogs): rol na 'du shes zhes bya ba med de byis pa so so'i skye bo dag ni yang dag pa ma yin pa'i 'du shes kyis bcings pas 'dod chags su rnam par shes/zhe sdang du rnam par shes/gti mug tu rnam par shes/bu dang 
26b1(mdo sna tshogs): chung mar rnam par shes so//byis pa so so'i skye bo dag ni 'du shes kyi phung po la gnas te yang dag pa ma yin pa'i lam la chags pas 'du shes kyi phung pos 'khor ba na 'khor bar gyur te/yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du 'du shes kyi 
26b2(mdo sna tshogs): phung po yang dag pa ma yin par mi rtogs so//byis pa so so'i skyes bu dag ni bdag tu 'du shes/pha rol por 'du shes so//skyes pa dang bud med du 'du shes te 'du shes kyi phung pos bcings pas rnam par ma grol te/bdag 
26b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'du shes kyi phung po yin pa dang /bdag gi ba 'du shes kyi phung po yin bar chags shing 'dzin to//bdag cag ni byis pa so so'i skye bo'i rjes su bslab par mi bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams de 
26b4(mdo sna tshogs): ltar yang dag par ji lta ba bzhin du 'du she kyi phung po la rtog pa na 'du shes kyi phung po de la 'du shes kyi phung por mi dmigs pa na smig rgyu ba'i phung po la smig rgyu ba'i phung por mi dmigs pa bzhin no//byang chub sems 
26b5(mdo sna tshogs): dpa'i 'du shes kyi phung po la smig rgyu ba nyid mthong bas 'das pa'i 'du shes kyi phung po mi chags/mi len/mi zhen/ma 'ongs pa'i 'du shes kyi phung bo la mi chags mi len/mi zhen/da ltar gyi 'du shes kyi phung po la 
26b6(mdo sna tshogs): mi gnas/bdag dang gzhan du rnam par mi rtog ste/'du shes kyi phung po 'gog pa la nan tan byed cing 'du shes kyi phung po'i lam ma skyes par khong du chud do//'du shes kyi phung po la ma 'ongs pa'i gnas dang /song ba'i 
26b7(mdo sna tshogs): gnas yod par mi mthong ste/phyin ci log dang ldan pa dang /sngon gyi las kyi rgyu las skyes te/da ltar gyi rkyen gyis rnam par bcings so//phung po med pa ni 'du shes kyi phung po'o//'du shes kyi phung po ma 'ongs pa'i 
26b8(mdo sna tshogs): gnas kyang med/song ba'i gnas kyang med par 'byed cing rtog na 'du shes kyi phung po ma skyes par khong du chud de/'du shes kyi phung po dgag par rnam par mi rtog pa ni 'du shes kyi tshor ba thams cad dgag pa'i phyir yang dag pa

27b1(mdo sna tshogs): byed pa dang /yid kyis 'du byed pa byas pa med de/mkhas pa dag ni 'du byed kyi phung po de la mi chags/mi len te/de ci'i phyir zhe na/lus kyis 'du byed pa rnams lus kyi nang na mi gnas/lus kyi phyir rol na mi gnas/
27b2(mdo sna tshogs): gnyi ga'i bar na mi gnas so//dag gis 'du byed pa dang /yid kyis 'dra byed pa yang de bzhin du nang na mi gnas/phyi rol na mi gnas/gnyi ga'i bar na mi gnas/'du byed kyi phung po la 'du byed kyi phung po'i mtshan ma

27b4(mdo sna tshogs): rnams byas par gyur to//lus kyi 'du byed pa dang /dag gis 'du byed pa dang/yid kyis 'du byed pa kun kyang yang dag par 'du byas pa ma yin/ci'ang 'du byed pa med de/yang dag pa ma yin pa las 'du byed pa/
27b5(mdo sna tshogs): phyin ci log tu 'du byed pa ste/de bas na 'du byed ma yin pa la 'du byed kyi phung po zhes btags so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/mkhas pa de dag gis ni 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid lam nges pas lus kyi 'du byed pa dang/dag gis 'du 
27b6(mdo sna tshogs): byed pa dang /yid kyis 'du byed pa dag gnas 'di na'am/gnas gzhan na'am/nang dam/phyi rol na gnas par ma nges pa'i phyir ro//lus dang /ngag dang /yid kyis 'du byedapa yang 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid du nges pa med/dmigs 
27b7(mdo sna tshogs): su med/brjod du med na/'du byed kyi phung po dmigs su yod pa'am brjod du yod pa lta ci smos te/de bas na phung po med pa ni 'du byed kyi phung po zhes bya'o//bus pa so so'i skye bo dag ni phyin ci log tu 'du shes bskyad nas 
27b8(mdo sna tshogs): lus dang /ngag dang /yin kyi 'du byed pa la chags te/'du byed kyi phung po la rnam par rtog pas 'du byed kyi phung pos bcings te 'khor ba na 'khor bar gyur to//bus pa so so'i skye bodag phyin ci log gi dbang du gyur pas lus dang 
28a1(mdo sna tshogs): ngag dang /yid kyi 'du byed bskyed de/de la 'dzin cing chags pas chos ma yin pa la chos su 'du shes/phung po med pa la phung por shes so//phyin ci log gi 'du byed la chags pas des rnam par bcings te 
28a2(mdo sna tshogs): lam rgyud lngar 'khor zhing rtag tu lus dang /ngag dang /yid kyi 'du bye da kyi rjes su spyod de yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du mi rtogs/yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin/'du byed kyi phung po ma rtogs pas lus dang /ngag dang /
28a3(mdo sna tshogs): yid kyi sgo nas 'du byed rnams bskyed par gyur to//byis pa so so'i skye bo dag phyin ci log la chags pa'i phyir/de kho na nyid ma yin pa'i chos la chags pa'i phyir/yang dag pa ma yin pa la chags pa'i phyir 'du byed kyi phung po zhes bya'o//
28a4(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten 'dzin byang chub sems dpa' rnams 'di la de ltar yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhi bzhin du brtags na 'du byed rnams kyi dngos po med de rab tu mthu chung ba rgyu dang rkyen mang po 'dus pa las 'du byed kyi phung po zhes/brjod cing brtags 
28a5(mdo sna tshogs): te de la yang dag par 'du byed kyi phung po med par rtogs so//phung po med pa ni 'du byed kyi phung po'o//thog ma nas ma skyes pa ni 'du byed kyi phung bo'o//rang bzhin med pa ni 'du byed kyi phur po'o//'du byed rnams snga ma'i 
28a6(mdo sna tshogs): mtha' yang dmigs su med/phyi ma'i mtha' yang dmigs su med par gyi mtha' yang dmigs su medade mi gnas pas 'du byed rnams ni skad cig gcig gis skye zhing 'gog pa'i chos can no//'jig rten 'dzin byang chub
28a7(mdo sna tshogs): sems dpa' rnams de ltar yang dag pa ji blta ba bzhin du 'du byed kyi phung po la stong pa nyid dang /dmigs su med pa dang /sra ba'i rang bzhin med pa dang /spu'i nyag ma tsam yang dmigs su med par rtogs so//de ltar 
28a8(mdo sna tshogs): bsams nas bus pa so so'i skye bo dag snying po med pa'i chos kyis rnam par bcings pa dang /'du byed kyi phung pos rnam par bcings pa dang/chags pas rnam par bcings te/lus dang /ngag dang /yid kyi sgo nas 
28b1(mdo sna tshogs): 'du byed pa bskyed do//bdag 'du byed pa dang bdag gi bar 'du byed pa bskyed de/de lta bu'i las bskyed pas 'du byed kyi phung pos rnam par bcings nas 'du byed kyi phung po nyid ma rig pa gti mug yin par mi shes te/'du byed kyi

28b3(mdo sna tshogs): sdug bsngal bar 'du byed/bar bar na bde ba'ang ma yin sdug bsngal yang ma yin par 'du byed do//gang zag des bde ba la 'du byed pas bde ba'i lus thob bo//sdug bsngal la 'du byed pas sdug bsngal gyi lus thob bo//
28b4(mdo sna tshogs): bde ba yang ma yin sdug bsngal ba yang ma yin pa la 'du byed pas bde ba yang ma yin sdug bsdal ba yang ma yin pa'i lus thob bo//gang zag des bde ba'i lus thob pas de la 'dod pa bskyed do//sdug bsngal gyi 
28b5(mdo sna tshogs): lus thob pas zhe sdang bskyed do//bde ba yang ma yin sdug bsal ba yang ma yin pa'i lus/thob pas gti mug bskyed do//gang zag des de ltar gdong chags dang /zhe sdang dang /gti mug gi dbang du gyur pas 'du byed kyi nyes 
28b6(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i skyon ma mthong nas/lus dang /ngag dang /yid kyi 'du byed rnam par ma dag go/gang zag de de ltar lus dang /ngag dang //yid kyi 'du byed rnam par mi dag pas rnam par ma dad pa'i lam du ltung bar gyur pa ni 'di lta ste/
28b7(mdo sna tshogs): sems can dmyal ba dang /byol song dang /yi dgas so//ji ste skad cig tsam de las thar te lha dang mir skyes na yang lus dang /ngag dang /yin kyi 'du byed la shin du chags te 'du byed kyi phung po la rab tu chags so//
28b8(mdo sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams ni de ltar yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rtogs so//bdag cag kyang byis pa so so'i skye bo'i rjes su bslab par mi bya'i/bdag cag gis lus dang ngag dang yid kyi 'du byed

29b2(mdo sna tshogs): kyi kun 'byung dang 'gog pa'i mtshan ma rtogs shing chud par 'gyur ro//'du byed rnams ma skyes pa'i mtshan nyid du reg kyang 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid shin du mi rtogs so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'jig rten 'dzin 'du byed kyi phung po la nges pa'i

29b4(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten 'dzin de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams 'du byed kyi phung po la 'jug pa yid la byed pa'i rnam par 'byed pa la rtog pa zhes bya'o//'jig rten 'dzin de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dba' sems

31b5(mdo sna tshogs): 'di lta ste/rnam par rtogs pa'i rnam par shes pa las 'jig rten 'byung ste/sems dang yid dang /rnam par shes pa 'dus pas na khams gsum 'di ni rnam par shes pa tsam mo//de ltar sems dang yid kyi rnam par shes pa ni gzugs 
31b6(mdo sna tshogs): med/phyogs med/chos kyi nang na mi gnas/chos kyi phyi rol na mi gnas so//bus pa so so'i skye bo dag ni yang dag pa ma yin pa dang ldan pas rnam par bcings pa'i phyir rnam par shes pa'i phung po la bdag dang /bdag gi bar

32a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//
32b1(mdo sna tshogs): gzugs kyi mi rtag pa'i mtshan nyid shes so//de ltar byang chub sems dpas gzugs la chags pa 'byung na de ma thag tu sel cing gcod do//de bzhin du yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed dang /

33a6(mdo sna tshogs): du rnam par shes pa'i 'gog pa'i lam shes so//gang zag de yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhan du rnam par shes pa'i phun po'i kun 'byung dang 'gog pa'i mtshan nyid rtogs nas rnam par shes pa'i phung ngo 'jig pa la mkhas pa dang /
33b1(mdo sna tshogs): mtshan ma thams cad yongs su gcod pa mkhas par gyur to//rnam par shes pa'i phung po'i kun 'byung dang 'gog pa'i mtshan nyid shes pa dang /rnam par shes pa'i phung po kun 'byung dang /'gog pa'i mtshan nyid rtogs so//byang chub sems

34a2(mdo sna tshogs): 'dzin nye bar len pa'i phung po'i skye zhing 'gog pa'i mtshan nid shin du phra ba rang sangs rgyas kyi shes rab kyis kyang shes par mi nus na nyan thos kyi shes rab kyis shes pa lta ci smos te/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das nyag cig gis

34a6(mdo sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das la gzhan gyi rjes su bltos pa'i shes rab mi mnga' ste/rang bzhin gyi ye shes dang /shes rab dang thabs mkhas pa brnyes pas bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par sangs rgyas so//
34a7(mdo sna tshogs): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi ye shes kyis ni mi chud pa med de/bcom ldan 'das kyi thogs pa med pa'i ye shes kyis chos thams cad la nges pa'i shes rab brnyes te chos thams cad dbang du gyur pa'i mthu yang brnyes so//
34a8(mdo sna tshogs): de ci'i phyir zhe na/bskal pa tshad med/dpag tu med/grangs med pa brgya stong snyed kyi bar du shin du zab mo'i chos mdzad pa'i phyir ro//'jig rten 'dzin byis pa so so'i skye bo dag ni nye bar len pa'i phung po lnga la rtog pa'i thabs 
34b1(mdo sna tshogs): kyang med na/nye bar len pa'i phung po lnga'i skye zhing 'gog pa'i mtshan nyid shin tu phra mo la rtog nus pa lta ci smos/de ci'i phyir zhe na/byis pa so so'i skye bo dag ni nye bar len pa'i phung po la yang mi shes/nye bar len pa'i phung bo lnga'i

34b7(mdo sna tshogs): dang 'byor/gzugs kyis bcings so//gzhan yang gzugs med pa'i phung po bzhi nye bar len te/tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed dang /rnam par shes par rnam par rtog cing ming du btags so//'jig rten 'dzin gal te 
34b8(mdo sna tshogs): sangs rgyas med na sems can rnams shes pa med mthong ba med pas nye bar len pa'i phung po lnga la yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rtog mi nus so//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten du gshegs nas sems can gzugs la 
35a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//rten cing gnas pa gzhig pa dang /tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed dang /rnam par shes pa la rten cing gnas pa gzhig pas dang /bsdu ba'i mtshan nyid gzhig pa'i phyir sangs rgyas bcom

35a3(mdo sna tshogs): ming dang gzugs kyi mtshan ma bsgrub pa'i phyir nye bar len pa'i phung po lnga bshad do//khyod so so'i skye bo rnams kyis mi brtan pa'i snying po med pa'i nye bar len pa'i phung po dang lnga 'di la mi gnas mi chags par bya'o//'jig 
35a4(mdo sna tshogs): rten 'dzin de bzhin gshegs pas ci'i phyir nye bar len pa'i 'phung po bshad ce na//'jig rten 'dzin bus pa so so'i skye bo dag phyin ci log las skyes te/ma rig pa'i dra bas glags pas 'khor ba na 'khor bar gyur to//cis 'khor

35a7(mdo sna tshogs): skye bo dag brten pa'i gnas ni nye bar len pa'i phung po lnga yin no//'jig rten 'dzin bus so so'i skye bo dag ni skyes nas ldongs te phung po lnga ci yin pa mi shes/phung po lnga gang nas 'ongs pa'ang mi shes so//phung po
35a8(mdo sna tshogs): lnga'i yang dag pa ji lta ba mi shes pas phung po la chags shing 'dzin te de bas na nye bar len pa'i phung po/zhes bya'o//de la len pa'ang med la len par bya ba yang dmigs su med de/phyin ci log la chags shing yang 
35b1(mdo sna tshogs): dag pa ma yin par kun du rtog pas bcings te/ma rig pa dang /gti mug gis rmongs pas bdag len/bdag gi bar len/bdag dang gzhan gyi bar len te/de bas na nye bar len pa'i phung po zhes bya'o//phung po lnga la
