40b8(mdo sna tshogs): su med de/gzugs kyi khams ni ma 'dus pa ma byas pa'o//gzugs la nges pa'i mtshan nyid med pa'i phyir gzugs kyi khams shes bya'o//gzugs la dngos po med de rnam par rtog pa'ang med na gzugs kyi khams shes 
41a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//bya ba lta ci smos te/gzugs kyi khams shes bya ba ni dngos po med pas gzugs kyi mtshan nyid ni gzugs kyi nang na yang mi gnas/gzugs kyi phyi rol na yang mi gnas/gnyi ga'i bar na yang mi gnas te/
41a2(mdo sna tshogs): rnam par brtags pa dang /gzugs mngon du snang ba'i gnas na yod la mig gi dbang po yang rnam par dag cing yid kyi rnam par shes pa dang ldan pa las gzugs mngon par snang ste/gzugs kyi mtshan nyid ces bya'o//dper na me 
41a3(mdo sna tshogs): long gi nang gi gzugs brnyan dang 'dra ste/me long yongs su dag na gzugs kyi mtshan nyid mngon par snang bar 'gyur ro//gzugs la nges pa'i mtshan nyid med de/me long gi nang na mi med bzhin du gzugs kyi gzugs brnyan snang ngo //
41a4(mdo sna tshogs): de yang phyi rol na me long yod na nang nas gzugs kyi mtshan nyid 'byung ngo //de bzhin du mig gi dbang po rnam par dag pa na gzugs snang ba'i gnas na 'dug pa la dmigs nas me long gzugs brnyan dang 'dra ba la gzugs kyi mtshan 
41a5(mdo sna tshogs): nyid ces brtags so//gzugs la khams med/dbyibs kyi mtshan ma med/nges pa'i mtshan nyid med pas gzugs kyi mtshan nyid ces pa'o//gzugs kyi mtshan nyid la bdag med pa'i phyir gzugs kyi mtshan nyid ces
41a6(mdo sna tshogs): sems can gyi rjes su ji lta ji lta ba de ltar de bzhin du shes pa'i phyir gzugs kyi mtshananyid ces bya'o//gang su zhig gzugs kyi mtshan nyid shes na gzugs med pa'i mtshan nyid kyang shes so//gzugs kyi mtshan nyi
41a7(mdo sna tshogs): da lam skyes pa'i khams su shes na/gzugs kyi mtshan nyid ma byas pa'i khams su shes so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/gzugs kyi mtshan nyidade 'das pa ma yin/ma 'ongs pa ma yin/da ltar gyi ma yin/mtshan nyid ci'ang med 
41a8(mdo sna tshogs): pa bstan pa/yang dag pa ma yin pa'i mtshan nyid bstan pa/bla dgas su btags pa'i mtshan nyid bstan pa ste/de dag ni gzugs kyi khams shes bya'o//de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gzugs kyi khams rnam par 
41b1(mdo sna tshogs): 'byed pa la rtog pa ste/byang chub sems dpas de nas mig gi rnam par shes pa'i mtshan nyid rnam par 'byed pa la rtog go/'di lta ste/mig gi rnam par shes pa la mig gi rnam par shes pa med/mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams la 
41b2(mdo sna tshogs): med/rtag pa'i mtshan nyid med do//mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khyams la dngos po med/nges pa'i chos med do//mig gi rnam pa shes pa'i khams ni bstan du med pa'o//mig gi rnam par shes ba'i khams ni 'dus pa ma yin 
41b3(mdo sna tshogs): gyis pa ma yin te/dngos po'i rtsa ba med do//de'ang sngon gyi las kyi rgyu dang rkyen dang /da ltar gyi rkyen dang gzugs la rag lus pa'i phyir 'di rnam par shes pa'i khamasashes bya'o//byis pa so so'i skye bo phyin ci log gi sems kyi 
41b4(mdo sna tshogs): rjes su song bas mig gi rnam par shes ba'i khyams shes bya'o//de la 'phags pa rnams kyis ni khams med par khong du chud cing rtogs so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/mig gi rnam par shes la des pa med pa'i phyir ro//
41b5(mdo sna tshogs): de ni rkyen rnams las skyes te/rkyen la rag lus pa'i phyir mig gi rnam par shes pa'i kams shes bya'o//rnam par shes pa'i spyod yul gyi gnas ni mig gi rnam par shes pa khams shes bya'o//de ltar nges pa med pa'i 
41b6(mdo sna tshogs): phyir na nges pa'i mtshan nyid ces bshad do//ma skyes pa'i phyir/yang dag pa ma yin pa bstan pa'i phyir ro//gzugs kyi mtshan mar rnam par rtog pa'i phyir/rkyen rnams bstan pa'i phyir mig gi rnam pa shes pa'i khams 
41b7(mdo sna tshogs): shes bshad do//sems can dag ji ltar shes pa'i rjes su de bzhin gshegs pas thabs kyis rnam par phye ste/gcig pa dang tha dad pa'i mtshan nyid gzhom pa'i phyir de lta bu'i mtshan nyid bshad do//rnam par shes 
41b8(mdo sna tshogs): pa la nges pa'i mtshan nyid med de mig gi dbang po rnam par dag pas gzugs kyi mtshan nyid shes te/chos gnyis 'dus pa'i phyir mig gi rnam bar shes pa'i khams shes bshad do//mig gi rnam par shes pa'i yang dag 
42a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//pa'i mtshan nyid bstan pa'i phyir mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams zhes bshad do//mig gi rnam par shes pa'i lams ni mig gi spyod yul gyi gnas te/gzugs su rnam par shes pa ni mig gi rnam par 
42a2(mdo sna tshogs): shes pa'i khams de'ang khams med par bshad pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/mkhas pa dag gis mig gi rnam par shes pa'i+i khams la mig gi rnam par shes pa khams su dmigs su med de/mig gi rnam par shes pa'i+-i khams 
42a3(mdo sna tshogs): la mig gi rnam par shes pa'-i khams su dmigs su medapas dngos por mi rnyed do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/nges pa'i mtshan nyid med pa ni mig gi rnam par shes pas khams so//mig gi rnam par shes ba'i khams ni brtags pa 
42a4(mdo sna tshogs): tsam du zad do//khams shes bshad pa de yang mi len pa'i don bshad par zad do//mthong bar bya ba'i gnas yod pas mig gi rnam par shes dang yid kyi las las mig gi rnam par shes par mtshan nyid skyes pas mig gi 
42a5(mdo sna tshogs): rnam par shes pa zhes bya'o//mig gi khams dang //gzugs kyi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes ba'i+i khams dang /dngos po gsum 'dus pa las rkyen rnams kyi mtshan nyid shes te/de ni khams kyi don las dben pa'o//
42a6(mdo sna tshogs): 'di lta ste/mig gi khams dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /mag gi rnam par shes pa'i khams rnam grangs de rnyed kyis na sems can rnams yang dag pa'i lam la 'jug par 'gyur te/'di la yang dag par na mig gi khams med 
42a7(mdo sna tshogs): gzugs kyi khams med/mig gi rnam par shes pa'i lams med de/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis mtshan nyid rnams shes pa dang /mthong ba dang /rnam par 'byed pa'i thabs su gsungs te/mtshan nyid gsungs pa 
42a8(mdo sna tshogs): 'di la gang zag su zhig chud cing rtogs na khams gsum po la khams med par shes so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/khams rnams la khams kyi mtshan nyid med pa'i phyir khams rnams la mtshan nyid du mi dmigs pa'i phyir ro//
42b1(mdo sna tshogs): rna ba'i khams dang /sgra'i khams dang /rna ba'i rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /sna'i khams dang /dri'i khams dang /sna'i rnam par shes pa'i khams/lce'i khams dang /ro'i khams dang /lce'i rnam par shes pa'i khams
42b2(mdo sna tshogs): dang /lus kyi khams dang /reg bya'i khams dang /lus kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams kyang gzhung 'dis shes par bya'o//'jig rten 'dzin de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams yid kyi khams
42b3(mdo sna tshogs): rnam par 'byed pa la rtog pa zhe na/byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam du yid kyi khams la dngos po ci yang med pa'i phyir nges pa'i mtshan nyid med do//yid kyi khams la yid kyi khams shes bya ba med nges pa'i khams med do//
42b4(mdo sna tshogs): yid kyi khams shes bya ba na dper na sa bon sa chen po la rmos nas ser gyis rlan te nyi mas dros par byas shing rlung gis bskyod na myu gu bags kyis 'byung ste/myu gu yang sa bon las ma skyes la/sa bon yang myu gu dang gcig
42b5(mdo sna tshogs): pa ma yin te/myu gu skyes nas sa bon yang 'jig ste/sa bon yang myu gu las gzhan ma yin la myu gu yang sa bon las gzhan ma yin te/myu gu'i nang nas sa bon med pa bzhin yid kyi khams kyang de dang 'dra ste/yid kyi las
42b6(mdo sna tshogs): bskyed pa'i phyir yid kyi rnam par shes pas mtshon pas ni sa bon gyis myu gu mtshon pa bzhin te/yid kyi khams shes brtags so//yid kyi khams las gud na yid dang yid kyi khams shes bya ba maid/yid du mi shes kyang bla
42b7(mdo sna tshogs): dgas su yid kyi khams zhes brtags so//yid kyi kha mas shes bya ba de yid kyi nang na mi gnas/yid kyi phyi rol na yang mi gnas/gnyi ga'i bar na mi gnas te/sngon gyi las kyi rgyu dang rkyen gyi phyir rnam par shes pas bskyed
42b8(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i phyir/yid kyi las kyi phyir/dmigs par bya ba shes pa'i phyir/mtshan nyid du btags pa 'dus pa'i phyir/da ltar gyi dmigs pa skye ba'i phyir yid kyi khams shes bya ste/de ni ma nges pa'i yid kyi las kyi mtshan nyid dang
43a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//rgyu dang rkyen mang po 'dus pa'i mtshan nyid de//yid kyi khams ni mang po'i 'dus pa las skye bas sems can ji ltar shes pa'i rjes su 'jug go/don dam pa la ni yid kyi khams shes bya ba med do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/dngos

43a4(mdo sna tshogs): la rang bzhin med pas rang bzhin nyid dmigs su med de/nges pa med pa'i khams ni chos kyi khams so//chos kyi khams ni dngos por dmigs su med pa'i phyir nges pa'i dngos por dmigs su med/skye bo phyin ci log tu

43a6(mdo sna tshogs): chos kyi khams la chos kyi khams med de/chos kyi khams shes bya ba ni rkyen mang po 'dus pa las skyes so//rkyen 'dus pa las skyes pa'i chos la ni rang bzhin med do//rgyu dang rkyen la rang bzhin med pa'i phyir/rgyu dang rkyen ni 
43a7(mdo sna tshogs): mang po 'dus pa'i phyin ci log gi rgyun las skyes so//de bzhin gshegs pas sems can yongs su smin par bya ba'i phyir kun rdzob kyi tshig tu brjod pas khams med pa'i chos bstan pa'i phyir chos kyi khams gsungs 
43a8(mdo sna tshogs): so//chos kyi khams ni nang na mi gnas/phyi rol na mi gnas/gnyi ga'i bar na mi gnas kyang so so'i skye bo dag dge ba dang mi dge ba'i chos shes shing mthong ngo //chos kyi khams kyis chos thams cad shes pa dang mthong ba med 
43b1(mdo sna tshogs): par bshad pa ni mthar stong pa nyid kyi mtshan nyid kyi phyir mthar stong pa nyid du bshad pa ni chos kyi khams yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/ci'ang med pa ni chos kyi khams te/chos kyi khams la yod ces pa'i mtshan ma nges pa med pa'i 
43b2(mdo sna tshogs): phyir ro//'di lta ste/nam mkha' la nges pa'i mtshan nyid med de/nam mkha' zhes bya ba bzhin chos kyi khams kyang de dang 'dra ste nges pa'i mtshan ma med do//chos kyi mtshan ma gzhom pa'i phyir chos kyi khams shes bshad de/khams 
43b3(mdo sna tshogs): med pa ni chos kyi khams so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/chos kyi khams ni 'das pa la mi gnas ma 'ongs pa la mi gnas/da ltar la mi gnas te/rkyen mang po la rag lus pas rkyen dang phrad pa'i phyir chos kyi khams zhes rnam grangs su 
43b4(mdo sna tshogs): btags so//sems can gyis ji ltar chos kyi khams shes btags so//mkhas pa dag khams med pa la chos kyi khams su shes te/chos kyi khams ni gcig pa ma yin tha dad pa ma yin no//chos kyi khams la chos kyi khams 
43b5(mdo sna tshogs): kyi mtshan ma mang po yang med/cung zad kyang med de/khams bsten pa'i thabs kyis chos kyi khams la khams shes bshad do//gang rnal 'byor pa dag chos kyi khams rtogs shing chud pa ni khams med par shes shing mthong ngo //
43b6(mdo sna tshogs): khams gsum gyi chos kyi khams kyi rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan ma med de/rgyu dang rkyen mang po 'dus pa las chos su rnam par rtog pas chos kyi khams shes bshad de/chos su rnam par bye ste mtshan nyid nges pa med pa bstan pa'i phyir 
43b7(mdo sna tshogs): chos kyi khams shes bshad do//mkhas pa dag ni chos kyi khams la mtshan mar blta ba'i phyir chos skyes par mthong ba ma yin te/gang skyes pa'i chos de nyid ma skyes pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/chos kyi khams la mtshan ma med pa'i 
43b8(mdo sna tshogs): phyir ro//mkhas pa dag ni chos mtshan nyid la mtshan ma med par rtogs shing chud pas chos kyi khams la rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan med pa ni mtshan ma med pa rnam par rtog pa med pa'i phyir chos kyi khams shes gsungs so//

44a7(mdo sna tshogs): shes pa zhes gsungs so//bus pa so so'i skye bo'i rjes su bsnyeg pa'i phyir yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i+i khams shes gsungs so//'phags pa rnams kyis ni khams ma yin pa la yid kyi rnam par shes pa' khams su shes

310b1(mdo sna tshogs): pa/'jam dpal de de bzhin te/chos kyi rnam grangs 'di thos pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab po//'on kyang sbyor ba med pa la la de thos nas mos par mi 'gyur du 'ong ba'i phyir ro//de bzhin gshegs pa lung mi ston to//de nas bcom ldan 'das
310b2(mdo sna tshogs): la 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po byams pas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das slad ma'i dus lnga brgya pa tha ma la ci nas kyang chos kyi rnam grangs 'di rgyas par 'gyur
310b3(mdo sna tshogs): zhing/skye bo mang po'i sug par phyin par 'gyur ba dang/de la bdud dam bdud kyi ris kyi lha dag gis glags rnyed par mi 'gyur ba de ltar byin gyis brlab tu gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis chos kyi rnam grangs 'di byin gyis
310b4(mdo sna tshogs): brlabs pa'i phyir g.yas g.yon du gzigs so//bcom ldan 'das kyis de ltar gzigs ma thag tu de'i tshe phyogs bcur sangs rgyas kyi zhing gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed rnam pa drug tu g.yos par gyur/rab tu g.yos
310b5(mdo sna tshogs): par gyur/kun du g.yos par gyur to//de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis chosakyi rnam grangs 'di byin gyi sa brlab pa mdzad do/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das gzhan dag gis kyang 'jig rten gyi khams gang gA'i klung
310b6(mdo sna tshogs): gi bye ma snyed dag nas chos kyi    rnam grangs 'di byin gyis brlabs so//bcom ldan 'das kyi chos kyi rnam grangs 'di bstan pa'i tshe phyogs bcu na gang gA'i klung gi bye mji snyed yod pa de bas kyang shin du mang bar
310b7(mdo sna tshogs): sems can rnams kyis mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob par gyur to//gang dag nyan thos nyid la gnas par gyur pa dang/slob pa'i sa 'am/mi slob pa'i sa la gnas par gyur pa de dag ni smos kyang mi smos so//de nas
310b8(mdo sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bos 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das chos kyi rnam grangs 'di ming ci zhes bgyi/ji ltar gzung bar bgyi/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dga' bo de bas na
311a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//chos kyi   rnam grangs 'di tshos thams cad 'byung ba med pa zhes bya bar zung shig/bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa dang /
311a2(mdo sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpa' byams pa dang /lha'i bu pad mo rnam par rol pa'i mngon par shes pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs chen po dang /dge slong lnga brgya tsam po de dag dang /tshe dang ldan pa kun 
311a3(mdo sna tshogs): dga' bo dang /lha dang /mi dang /lha ma yin dang /dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten bcom ldan 'das kyis             gsungs pa la yid rangs te mngon par dga'o//'phags pa chos thams cad 'byung ba medapa 
311a4(mdo sna tshogs): zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo//rdzogs s+ho//lo ts+tsha ba ban d+he rin chen mtshos bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa//ye d+ha rmA he tu pra b+ha bA he tu n+te ShAn+ta thA ga to h+yab data/te ShAny+tsa yo ni ro d+ha e baM bA dI ma hA shra ma Na//maMg+ha laM b+ha waM tu//
